## 📁 طبقة AI Provider

### `src/lib/ai/provider.ts`
```ts
import type {
  AIProvider,
  AIGenerateOptions,
  AIStreamChunk,
  AIUsage,
  AIModelInfo,
} from '@/types/ai';

export type { AIProvider, AIGenerateOptions, AIStreamChunk, AIUsage, AIModelInfo };

/**
 * Registry of available providers.
 * Adding a new provider is: implement the interface + register it here.
 */
export type ProviderName = 'deepseek' | 'openai' | 'gemini';

export interface ProviderFactoryConfig {
  name: ProviderName;
  apiKey: string;
  baseUrl?: string;
}

export class ProviderError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    readonly code: string,
    readonly status?: number,
    readonly provider?: ProviderName,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ProviderError';
  }
}
```

### `src/lib/ai/adapters/deepseek.ts`
```ts
import type {
  AIProvider,
  AIGenerateOptions,
  AIStreamChunk,
  AIModelInfo,
} from '@/types/ai';
import { ProviderError } from '../provider';

const DEFAULT_BASE_URL = 'https://api.deepseek.com/v1';
const DEFAULT_MODEL_INFO = {
  contextWindow: 64000,
  supportsStreaming: true,
  supportsVision: false,
};

/**
 * DeepSeek adapter — OpenAI-compatible API.
 */
export class DeepSeekProvider implements AIProvider {
  readonly name = 'deepseek';

  constructor(
    private readonly apiKey: string,
    private readonly baseUrl: string = DEFAULT_BASE_URL,
  ) {
    if (!apiKey) throw new ProviderError('DeepSeek API key missing', 'missing_api_key');
  }

  async generateResponse(
    options: AIGenerateOptions,
  ): Promise<{ content: string; usage: { promptTokens: number; completionTokens: number; totalTokens: number } }> {
    const res = await this.request('/chat/completions', {
      model: options.model,
      messages: options.messages,
      temperature: options.temperature ?? 0.7,
      max_tokens: options.maxTokens,
      stream: false,
    }, options.signal);

    const data = (await res.json()) as {
      choices: Array<{ message: { content: string } }>;
      usage?: { prompt_tokens: number; completion_tokens: number; total_tokens: number };
    };

    const content = data.choices[0]?.message?.content ?? '';
    const usage = {
      promptTokens: data.usage?.prompt_tokens ?? 0,
      completionTokens: data.usage?.completion_tokens ?? 0,
      totalTokens: data.usage?.total_tokens ?? 0,
    };
    return { content, usage };
  }

  async *streamResponse(options: AIGenerateOptions): AsyncIterable<AIStreamChunk> {
    const res = await this.request('/chat/completions', {
      model: options.model,
      messages: options.messages,
      temperature: options.temperature ?? 0.7,
      max_tokens: options.maxTokens,
      stream: true,
      stream_options: { include_usage: true },
    }, options.signal);

    if (!res.body) throw new ProviderError('No response body', 'no_body', res.status, 'deepseek');

    const reader = res.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
    let buffer = '';
    let usage: AIStreamChunk['usage'];

    try {
      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });

        const lines = buffer.split('\n');
        buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';

        for (const raw of lines) {
          const line = raw.trim();
          if (!line || !line.startsWith('data:')) continue;
          const payload = line.slice(5).trim();
          if (payload === '[DONE]') {
            yield { delta: '', done: true, usage };
            return;
          }
          try {
            const json = JSON.parse(payload) as {
              choices?: Array<{ delta?: { content?: string } }>;
              usage?: { prompt_tokens: number; completion_tokens: number; total_tokens: number };
            };
            const delta = json.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content ?? '';
            if (json.usage) {
              usage = {
                promptTokens: json.usage.prompt_tokens,
                completionTokens: json.usage.completion_tokens,
                totalTokens: json.usage.total_tokens,
              };
            }
            if (delta) yield { delta, done: false };
          } catch {
            // ignore malformed line
          }
        }
      }
      yield { delta: '', done: true, usage };
    } finally {
      reader.releaseLock();
    }
  }

  async countTokens(text: string): Promise<number> {
    // Rough estimate: 1 token ≈ 4 characters (works for Arabic + Latin).
    return Math.ceil(text.length / 4);
  }

  async getModelInfo(model: string): Promise<AIModelInfo> {
    return {
      slug: model,
      name: model,
      provider: 'deepseek',
      contextWindow: DEFAULT_MODEL_INFO.contextWindow,
      supportsStreaming: DEFAULT_MODEL_INFO.supportsStreaming,
      supportsVision: DEFAULT_MODEL_INFO.supportsVision,
    };
  }

  private async request(
    path: string,
    body: unknown,
    signal?: AbortSignal,
  ): Promise<Response> {
    const res = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}${path}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
      signal,
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
      const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
      throw new ProviderError(
        `DeepSeek request failed: ${res.status} ${text.slice(0, 200)}`,
        'provider_error',
        res.status,
        'deepseek',
      );
    }
    return res;
  }
}
```

### `src/lib/ai/adapters/openai.ts`
```ts
import type { AIProvider, AIGenerateOptions, AIStreamChunk, AIModelInfo } from '@/types/ai';
import { ProviderError } from '../provider';

const DEFAULT_BASE_URL = 'https://api.openai.com/v1';

export class OpenAIProvider implements AIProvider {
  readonly name = 'openai';

  constructor(
    private readonly apiKey: string,
    private readonly baseUrl: string = DEFAULT_BASE_URL,
  ) {
    if (!apiKey) throw new ProviderError('OpenAI API key missing', 'missing_api_key');
  }

  async generateResponse(options: AIGenerateOptions) {
    const res = await this.request('/chat/completions', {
      model: options.model,
      messages: options.messages,
      temperature: options.temperature ?? 0.7,
      max_tokens: options.maxTokens,
    }, options.signal);

    const data = (await res.json()) as {
      choices: Array<{ message: { content: string } }>;
      usage?: { prompt_tokens: number; completion_tokens: number; total_tokens: number };
    };

    return {
      content: data.choices[0]?.message?.content ?? '',
      usage: {
        promptTokens: data.usage?.prompt_tokens ?? 0,
        completionTokens: data.usage?.completion_tokens ?? 0,
        totalTokens: data.usage?.total_tokens ?? 0,
      },
    };
  }

  async *streamResponse(options: AIGenerateOptions): AsyncIterable<AIStreamChunk> {
    const res = await this.request('/chat/completions', {
      model: options.model,
      messages: options.messages,
      temperature: options.temperature ?? 0.7,
      max_tokens: options.maxTokens,
      stream: true,
      stream_options: { include_usage: true },
    }, options.signal);

    if (!res.body) throw new ProviderError('No response body', 'no_body', res.status, 'openai');

    const reader = res.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
    let buffer = '';
    let usage: AIStreamChunk['usage'];

    try {
      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const lines = buffer.split('\n');
        buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
        for (const raw of lines) {
          const line = raw.trim();
          if (!line.startsWith('data:')) continue;
          const payload = line.slice(5).trim();
          if (payload === '[DONE]') {
            yield { delta: '', done: true, usage };
            return;
          }
          try {
            const json = JSON.parse(payload) as {
              choices?: Array<{ delta?: { content?: string } }>;
              usage?: { prompt_tokens: number; completion_tokens: number; total_tokens: number };
            };
            const delta = json.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content ?? '';
            if (json.usage) {
              usage = {
                promptTokens: json.usage.prompt_tokens,
                completionTokens: json.usage.completion_tokens,
                totalTokens: json.usage.total_tokens,
              };
            }
            if (delta) yield { delta, done: false };
          } catch {
            /* ignore */
          }
        }
      }
      yield { delta: '', done: true, usage };
    } finally {
      reader.releaseLock();
    }
  }

  async countTokens(text: string): Promise<number> {
    return Math.ceil(text.length / 4);
  }

  async getModelInfo(model: string): Promise<AIModelInfo> {
    return {
      slug: model,
      name: model,
      provider: 'openai',
      contextWindow: 128000,
      supportsStreaming: true,
      supportsVision: true,
    };
  }

  private async request(path: string, body: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal) {
    const res = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}${path}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
      signal,
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
      throw new ProviderError(
        `OpenAI request failed: ${res.status} ${text.slice(0, 200)}`,
        'provider_error',
        res.status,
        'openai',
      );
    }
    return res;
  }
}
```

### `src/lib/ai/adapters/gemini.ts`
```ts
import type { AIProvider, AIGenerateOptions, AIStreamChunk, AIModelInfo } from '@/types/ai';
import { ProviderError } from '../provider';

const DEFAULT_BASE_URL = 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta';

type GeminiContent = {
  role: 'user' | 'model';
  parts: Array<{ text: string }>;
};

export class GeminiProvider implements AIProvider {
  readonly name = 'gemini';

  constructor(
    private readonly apiKey: string,
    private readonly baseUrl: string = DEFAULT_BASE_URL,
  ) {
    if (!apiKey) throw new ProviderError('Gemini API key missing', 'missing_api_key');
  }

  async generateResponse(options: AIGenerateOptions) {
    const { contents, systemInstruction } = this.mapMessages(options.messages);
    const res = await this.request(`/models/${options.model}:generateContent`, {
      contents,
      systemInstruction,
      generationConfig: {
        temperature: options.temperature ?? 0.7,
        maxOutputTokens: options.maxTokens,
      },
    }, options.signal);

    const data = (await res.json()) as {
      candidates?: Array<{ content?: { parts?: Array<{ text?: string }> } }>;
      usageMetadata?: {
        promptTokenCount?: number;
        candidatesTokenCount?: number;
        totalTokenCount?: number;
      };
    };

    const content = data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.map((p) => p.text ?? '').join('') ?? '';
    return {
      content,
      usage: {
        promptTokens: data.usageMetadata?.promptTokenCount ?? 0,
        completionTokens: data.usageMetadata?.candidatesTokenCount ?? 0,
        totalTokens: data.usageMetadata?.totalTokenCount ?? 0,
      },
    };
  }

  async *streamResponse(options: AIGenerateOptions): AsyncIterable<AIStreamChunk> {
    const { contents, systemInstruction } = this.mapMessages(options.messages);
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/models/${options.model}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key=${this.apiKey}`;

    const res = await fetch(url, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        contents,
        systemInstruction,
        generationConfig: {
          temperature: options.temperature ?? 0.7,
          maxOutputTokens: options.maxTokens,
        },
      }),
      signal: options.signal,
    });

    if (!res.ok || !res.body) {
      const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
      throw new ProviderError(
        `Gemini request failed: ${res.status} ${text.slice(0, 200)}`,
        'provider_error',
        res.status,
        'gemini',
      );
    }

    const reader = res.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
    let buffer = '';
    let usage: AIStreamChunk['usage'];

    try {
      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const lines = buffer.split('\n');
        buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
        for (const raw of lines) {
          const line = raw.trim();
          if (!line.startsWith('data:')) continue;
          const payload = line.slice(5).trim();
          if (!payload) continue;
          try {
            const json = JSON.parse(payload) as {
              candidates?: Array<{ content?: { parts?: Array<{ text?: string }> } }>;
              usageMetadata?: {
                promptTokenCount?: number;
                candidatesTokenCount?: number;
                totalTokenCount?: number;
              };
            };
            const delta =
              json.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.map((p) => p.text ?? '').join('') ?? '';
            if (json.usageMetadata) {
              usage = {
                promptTokens: json.usageMetadata.promptTokenCount ?? 0,
                completionTokens: json.usageMetadata.candidatesTokenCount ?? 0,
                totalTokens: json.usageMetadata.totalTokenCount ?? 0,
              };
            }
            if (delta) yield { delta, done: false };
          } catch {
            /* ignore */
          }
        }
      }
      yield { delta: '', done: true, usage };
    } finally {
      reader.releaseLock();
    }
  }

  async countTokens(text: string): Promise<number> {
    return Math.ceil(text.length / 4);
  }

  async getModelInfo(model: string): Promise<AIModelInfo> {
    return {
      slug: model,
      name: model,
      provider: 'gemini',
      contextWindow: 1_000_000,
      supportsStreaming: true,
      supportsVision: true,
    };
  }

  private mapMessages(messages: AIGenerateOptions['messages']) {
    const contents: GeminiContent[] = [];
    let systemInstruction: { parts: Array<{ text: string }> } | undefined;

    for (const msg of messages) {
      if (msg.role === 'system') {
        systemInstruction = { parts: [{ text: msg.content }] };
        continue;
      }
      contents.push({
        role: msg.role === 'assistant' ? 'model' : 'user',
        parts: [{ text: msg.content }],
      });
    }
    return { contents, systemInstruction };
  }

  private async request(path: string, body: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal) {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}${path}?key=${this.apiKey}`;
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
      signal,
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
      throw new ProviderError(
        `Gemini request failed: ${res.status} ${text.slice(0, 200)}`,
        'provider_error',
        res.status,
        'gemini',
      );
    }
    return res;
  }
}
```

### `src/lib/ai/factory.ts`
```ts
import type { AIProvider } from '@/types/ai';
import { ProviderError, type ProviderName } from './provider';
import { DeepSeekProvider } from './adapters/deepseek';
import { OpenAIProvider } from './adapters/openai';
import { GeminiProvider } from './adapters/gemini';

const registry = new Map<string, AIProvider>();
let _cache: Map<string, AIProvider> | null = null;

function readKey(name: ProviderName): string | undefined {
  switch (name) {
    case 'deepseek':
      return process.env.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY;
    case 'openai':
      return process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY;
    case 'gemini':
      return process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY;
  }
}

/** Resolves a provider instance from a model slug. */
export function getProviderForModel(modelSlug: string): AIProvider {
  const provider = inferProviderFromSlug(modelSlug);
  return getProvider(provider);
}

/** Explicit provider resolution. */
export function getProvider(name: ProviderName): AIProvider {
  if (_cache?.has(name)) return _cache.get(name)!;
  const key = readKey(name);
  if (!key) {
    throw new ProviderError(
      `Provider "${name}" is not configured (missing API key)`,
      'provider_not_configured',
      undefined,
      name,
    );
  }
  let instance: AIProvider;
  switch (name) {
    case 'deepseek':
      instance = new DeepSeekProvider(key);
      break;
    case 'openai':
      instance = new OpenAIProvider(key);
      break;
    case 'gemini':
      instance = new GeminiProvider(key);
      break;
  }
  _cache = _cache ?? new Map();
  _cache.set(name, instance);
  registry.set(name, instance);
  return instance;
}

/** Best-effort provider inference from a model slug. */
export function inferProviderFromSlug(slug: string): ProviderName {
  const s = slug.toLowerCase();
  if (s.startsWith('deepseek')) return 'deepseek';
  if (s.startsWith('gpt') || s.startsWith('o1') || s.startsWith('o3')) return 'openai';
  if (s.startsWith('gemini')) return 'gemini';
  // Fallback to configured default
  const fallback = (process.env.AI_DEFAULT_PROVIDER as ProviderName) ?? 'deepseek';
  return fallback;
}
```

## 📁 طبقة الأمان

### `src/lib/security/hash.ts`
```ts
import { createHash, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';

/** SHA-256 hex digest. */
export function sha256(input: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(input).digest('hex');
}

/** Generate an API key with a visible prefix + random secret. */
export function generateApiKey(): { prefix: string; secret: string; full: string; hash: string } {
  const prefix = `nados_${randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}`;
  const secret = randomBytes(24).toString('base64url');
  const full = `${prefix}_${secret}`;
  return { prefix, secret, full, hash: sha256(full) };
}

/** Constant-time comparison of two hex digests. */
export function safeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  const ba = Buffer.from(a, 'hex');
  const bb = Buffer.from(b, 'hex');
  if (ba.length !== bb.length) return false;
  return timingSafeEqual(ba, bb);
}

/** Hash IP address for audit logging without storing raw IP. */
export function hashIp(ip: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!ip) return null;
  const salt = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY?.slice(0, 16) ?? 'nados';
  return sha256(`${salt}:${ip}`).slice(0, 32);
}
```

### `src/lib/security/permissions.ts`
```ts
import type { UserRole } from '@/types/database';

export const ROLE_HIERARCHY: Record<UserRole, number> = {
  user: 0,
  admin: 10,
  owner: 20,
};

export function hasRole(userRole: UserRole | null | undefined, required: UserRole): boolean {
  if (!userRole) return false;
  return (ROLE_HIERARCHY[userRole] ?? -1) >= ROLE_HIERARCHY[required];
}

export function isAdmin(userRole: UserRole | null | undefined): boolean {
  return hasRole(userRole, 'admin');
}

export function isOwner(userRole: UserRole | null | undefined): boolean {
  return userRole === 'owner';
}
```

### `src/lib/security/rate-limit.ts`
```ts
/**
 * In-memory rate limiter. Suitable for Vercel serverless (per-instance).
 * For production-grade distributed limiting, swap with Upstash Redis.
 */

type Bucket = { count: number; resetAt: number };

const buckets = new Map<string, Bucket>();
const MAX_KEYS = 10_000;

function prune() {
  if (buckets.size <= MAX_KEYS) return;
  const now = Date.now();
  for (const [k, v] of buckets) {
    if (v.resetAt < now) buckets.delete(k);
    if (buckets.size <= MAX_KEYS * 0.8) break;
  }
}

export type RateLimitOptions = {
  key: string;
  limit: number;
  windowMs: number;
};

export type RateLimitResult = {
  allowed: boolean;
  remaining: number;
  resetAt: number;
};

export function rateLimit({ key, limit, windowMs }: RateLimitOptions): RateLimitResult {
  const now = Date.now();
  const existing = buckets.get(key);

  if (!existing || existing.resetAt < now) {
    buckets.set(key, { count: 1, resetAt: now + windowMs });
    prune();
    return { allowed: true, remaining: limit - 1, resetAt: now + windowMs };
  }

  existing.count += 1;
  const allowed = existing.count <= limit;
  return {
    allowed,
    remaining: Math.max(limit - existing.count, 0),
    resetAt: existing.resetAt,
  };
}

export const RATE_LIMITS = {
  login: { limit: 8, windowMs: 60_000 },
  register: { limit: 5, windowMs: 60_000 },
  passwordReset: { limit: 3, windowMs: 300_000 },
  chat: { limit: 30, windowMs: 60_000 },
  contact: { limit: 3, windowMs: 300_000 },
  apiKeysCreate: { limit: 5, windowMs: 60_000 },
} as const;
```

### `src/lib/security/sanitize.ts`
```ts
/** Strip control chars & neutralize leading formula characters. */
export function sanitizeText(input: string, maxLength?: number): string {
  let out = input.replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/g, '');
  // Prevent CSV formula injection if ever exported
  out = out.replace(/^[=+\-@]/, (c) => `'${c}`);
  if (maxLength && out.length > maxLength) out = out.slice(0, maxLength);
  return out.trim();
}

/** Escape HTML entities for safe plain-text interpolation. */
export function escapeHtml(input: string): string {
  return input
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}
```

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';

export const uuidSchema = z.string().uuid('معرّف غير صالح');

export const emailSchema = z
  .string()
  .min(3, 'البريد الإلكتروني مطلوب')
  .max(254)
  .email('بريد إلكتروني غير صالح')
  .transform((v) => v.toLowerCase().trim());

export const passwordSchema = z
  .string()
  .min(8, 'كلمة المرور يجب أن تكون 8 أحرف على الأقل')
  .max(72, 'كلمة المرور طويلة جدًا')
  .regex(/[A-Za-z]/, 'يجب أن تحتوي على حرف واحد على الأقل')
  .regex(/[0-9]/, 'يجب أن تحتوي على رقم واحد على الأقل');

export const usernameSchema = z
  .string()
  .min(3, 'اسم المستخدم قصير جدًا')
  .max(32, 'اسم المستخدم طويل جدًا')
  .regex(/^[a-z0-9_]+$/, 'فقط أحرف إنجليزية صغيرة، أرقام، وشرطة سفلية')
  .transform((v) => v.toLowerCase());

export const paginationSchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(20),
});

export const idParamSchema = z.object({ id: uuidSchema });
```

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { emailSchema, passwordSchema, usernameSchema } from './common';

export const registerSchema = z.object({
  fullName: z.string().min(2, 'الاسم قصير جدًا').max(80).trim(),
  email: emailSchema,
  username: usernameSchema,
  password: passwordSchema,
});

export const loginSchema = z.object({
  email: emailSchema,
  password: z.string().min(1, 'كلمة المرور مطلوبة'),
});

export const forgotPasswordSchema = z.object({
  email: emailSchema,
});

export const resetPasswordSchema = z
  .object({
    password: passwordSchema,
    confirmPassword: z.string().min(1),
  })
  .refine((d) => d.password === d.confirmPassword, {
    message: 'كلمتا المرور غير متطابقتين',
    path: ['confirmPassword'],
  });

export type RegisterInput = z.infer<typeof registerSchema>;
export type LoginInput = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;
export type ForgotPasswordInput = z.infer<typeof forgotPasswordSchema>;
export type ResetPasswordInput = z.infer<typeof resetPasswordSchema>;
```

## 📁 Repositories

### `src/server/repositories/profiles.repo.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database, Tables, TablesUpdate } from '@/types/database';

type Client = SupabaseClient<Database>;

export class ProfilesRepository {
  constructor(private readonly db: Client) {}

  async findById(userId: string): Promise<Tables<'profiles'> | null> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('profiles')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', userId)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async findByUsername(username: string): Promise<Tables<'profiles'> | null> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('profiles')
      .select('*')
      .eq('username', username)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async update(userId: string, patch: TablesUpdate<'profiles'>): Promise<Tables<'profiles'>> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('profiles')
      .update(patch)
      .eq('id', userId)
      .select()
      .single();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async list(params: { page: number; pageSize: number; search?: string; role?: string }) {
    const from = (params.page - 1) * params.pageSize;
    const to = from + params.pageSize - 1;

    let query = this.db
      .from('profiles')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact' })
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .range(from, to);

    if (params.search) {
      query = query.or(
        `email.ilike.%${params.search}%,username.ilike.%${params.search}%,full_name.ilike.%${params.search}%`,
      );
    }
    if (params.role) query = query.eq('role', params.role);

    const { data, error, count } = await query;
    if (error) throw error;
    return { items: data ?? [], total: count ?? 0 };
  }

  async setActive(userId: string, isActive: boolean) {
    const { error } = await this.db
      .from('profiles')
      .update({ is_active: isActive })
      .eq('id', userId);
    if (error) throw error;
  }

  async setRole(userId: string, role: Tables<'profiles'>['role']) {
    const { error } = await this.db.from('profiles').update({ role }).eq('id', userId);
    if (error) throw error;
  }
}
```

### `src/server/repositories/conversations.repo.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database, Tables, TablesInsert, TablesUpdate } from '@/types/database';

type Client = SupabaseClient<Database>;

export class ConversationsRepository {
  constructor(private readonly db: Client) {}

  async findById(id: string): Promise<Tables<'conversations'> | null> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('conversations')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', id)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async list(params: {
    userId: string;
    page: number;
    pageSize: number;
    includeArchived?: boolean;
    onlyArchived?: boolean;
    onlyFavorites?: boolean;
    search?: string;
  }) {
    const from = (params.page - 1) * params.pageSize;
    const to = from + params.pageSize - 1;

    let query = this.db
      .from('conversations')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact' })
      .eq('user_id', params.userId)
      .order('is_favorite', { ascending: false })
      .order('updated_at', { ascending: false })
      .range(from, to);

    if (params.onlyArchived) query = query.eq('is_archived', true);
    else if (!params.includeArchived) query = query.eq('is_archived', false);

    if (params.onlyFavorites) query = query.eq('is_favorite', true);
    if (params.search) query = query.ilike('title', `%${params.search}%`);

    const { data, error, count } = await query;
    if (error) throw error;
    return { items: data ?? [], total: count ?? 0 };
  }

  async create(input: TablesInsert<'conversations'>): Promise<Tables<'conversations'>> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db.from('conversations').insert(input).select().single();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async update(id: string, patch: TablesUpdate<'conversations'>): Promise<Tables<'conversations'>> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('conversations')
      .update(patch)
      .eq('id', id)
      .select()
      .single();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const { error } = await this.db.from('conversations').delete().eq('id', id);
    if (error) throw error;
  }

  async countForUser(userId: string): Promise<number> {
    const { count, error } = await this.db
      .from('conversations')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .eq('is_archived', false);
    if (error) throw error;
    return count ?? 0;
  }
}
```

### `src/server/repositories/messages.repo.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database, Tables, TablesInsert, TablesUpdate } from '@/types/database';

type Client = SupabaseClient<Database>;

export class MessagesRepository {
  constructor(private readonly db: Client) {}

  async listByConversation(
    conversationId: string,
    params: { page?: number; pageSize?: number; order?: 'asc' | 'desc' } = {},
  ) {
    const page = params.page ?? 1;
    const pageSize = params.pageSize ?? 100;
    const order = params.order ?? 'asc';
    const from = (page - 1) * pageSize;
    const to = from + pageSize - 1;

    const { data, error, count } = await this.db
      .from('messages')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact' })
      .eq('conversation_id', conversationId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: order === 'asc' })
      .range(from, to);

    if (error) throw error;
    return { items: data ?? [], total: count ?? 0 };
  }

  async create(input: TablesInsert<'messages'>): Promise<Tables<'messages'>> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db.from('messages').insert(input).select().single();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async update(id: string, patch: TablesUpdate<'messages'>): Promise<Tables<'messages'>> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('messages')
      .update(patch)
      .eq('id', id)
      .select()
      .single();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const { error } = await this.db.from('messages').delete().eq('id', id);
    if (error) throw error;
  }

  async search(params: {
    userId: string;
    query: string;
    page: number;
    pageSize: number;
    model?: string;
    from?: string;
    to?: string;
  }) {
    const from = (params.page - 1) * params.pageSize;
    const to = from + params.pageSize - 1;

    let query = this.db
      .from('messages')
      .select('*, conversations!inner(id, title, model, is_archived)', { count: 'exact' })
      .eq('user_id', params.userId)
      .ilike('content', `%${params.query}%`)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .range(from, to);

    if (params.model) query = query.eq('model', params.model);
    if (params.from) query = query.gte('created_at', params.from);
    if (params.to) query = query.lte('created_at', params.to);

    const { data, error, count } = await query;
    if (error) throw error;
    return { items: data ?? [], total: count ?? 0 };
  }
}
```

### `src/server/repositories/usage.repo.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database, Tables, TablesInsert } from '@/types/database';

type Client = SupabaseClient<Database>;

export class UsageRepository {
  constructor(private readonly db: Client) {}

  async create(input: TablesInsert<'usage_records'>): Promise<Tables<'usage_records'>> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db.from('usage_records').insert(input).select().single();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async todayTotals(userId: string) {
    const dayStart = new Date();
    dayStart.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('usage_records')
      .select('prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, total_tokens')
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .gte('created_at', dayStart.toISOString());

    if (error) throw error;
    return (data ?? []).reduce(
      (acc, r) => {
        acc.messages += 1;
        acc.promptTokens += r.prompt_tokens ?? 0;
        acc.completionTokens += r.completion_tokens ?? 0;
        acc.totalTokens += r.total_tokens ?? 0;
        return acc;
      },
      { messages: 0, promptTokens: 0, completionTokens: 0, totalTokens: 0 },
    );
  }

  async userDaily(userId: string, days: number) {
    const since = new Date();
    since.setDate(since.getDate() - days);
    since.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('usage_records')
      .select('created_at, total_tokens, prompt_tokens, completion_tokens')
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .gte('created_at', since.toISOString())
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true });

    if (error) throw error;

    const byDay = new Map<string, { tokens: number; messages: number }>();
    for (const r of data ?? []) {
      const day = r.created_at.slice(0, 10);
      const cur = byDay.get(day) ?? { tokens: 0, messages: 0 };
      cur.tokens += r.total_tokens ?? 0;
      cur.messages += 1;
      byDay.set(day, cur);
    }
    return Array.from(byDay.entries()).map(([date, v]) => ({ date, ...v }));
  }

  async platformDaily(days: number) {
    const since = new Date();
    since.setDate(since.getDate() - days);

    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('usage_records')
      .select('created_at, total_tokens, user_id, model')
      .gte('created_at', since.toISOString());

    if (error) throw error;

    const byDay = new Map<string, { tokens: number; requests: number; users: Set<string> }>();
    const byModel = new Map<string, { tokens: number; requests: number }>();

    for (const r of data ?? []) {
      const day = r.created_at.slice(0, 10);
      const cur = byDay.get(day) ?? { tokens: 0, requests: 0, users: new Set<string>() };
      cur.tokens += r.total_tokens ?? 0;
      cur.requests += 1;
      cur.users.add(r.user_id);
      byDay.set(day, cur);

      const m = byModel.get(r.model) ?? { tokens: 0, requests: 0 };
      m.tokens += r.total_tokens ?? 0;
      m.requests += 1;
      byModel.set(r.model, m);
    }

    return {
      daily: Array.from(byDay.entries()).map(([date, v]) => ({
        date,
        tokens: v.tokens,
        requests: v.requests,
        users: v.users.size,
      })),
      byModel: Array.from(byModel.entries()).map(([model, v]) => ({ model, ...v })),
    };
  }
}
```

### `src/server/repositories/notifications.repo.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database, Tables, TablesInsert } from '@/types/database';

type Client = SupabaseClient<Database>;

export class NotificationsRepository {
  constructor(private readonly db: Client) {}

  async list(params: { userId: string; page: number; pageSize: number; unreadOnly?: boolean }) {
    const from = (params.page - 1) * params.pageSize;
    const to = from + params.pageSize - 1;

    let q = this.db
      .from('notifications')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact' })
      .eq('user_id', params.userId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .range(from, to);

    if (params.unreadOnly) q = q.eq('is_read', false);

    const { data, error, count } = await q;
    if (error) throw error;
    return { items: data ?? [], total: count ?? 0 };
  }

  async unreadCount(userId: string): Promise<number> {
    const { count, error } = await this.db
      .from('notifications')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .eq('is_read', false);
    if (error) throw error;
    return count ?? 0;
  }

  async create(input: TablesInsert<'notifications'>): Promise<Tables<'notifications'>> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db.from('notifications').insert(input).select().single();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async markRead(userId: string, ids: string[]): Promise<void> {
    const { error } = await this.db
      .from('notifications')
      .update({ is_read: true, read_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .in('id', ids);
    if (error) throw error;
  }

  async markAllRead(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    const { error } = await this.db
      .from('notifications')
      .update({ is_read: true, read_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .eq('is_read', false);
    if (error) throw error;
  }

  async delete(userId: string, id: string): Promise<void> {
    const { error } = await this.db
      .from('notifications')
      .delete()
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .eq('id', id);
    if (error) throw error;
  }
}
```

### `src/server/repositories/api-keys.repo.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database, Tables, TablesInsert } from '@/types/database';

type Client = SupabaseClient<Database>;

export class ApiKeysRepository {
  constructor(private readonly db: Client) {}

  async listForUser(userId: string) {
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('api_keys')
      .select('id, name, key_prefix, scopes, last_used_at, expires_at, revoked_at, created_at')
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    if (error) throw error;
    return data ?? [];
  }

  async create(input: TablesInsert<'api_keys'>): Promise<Tables<'api_keys'>> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db.from('api_keys').insert(input).select().single();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async findByPrefix(prefix: string): Promise<Tables<'api_keys'> | null> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from('api_keys')
      .select('*')
      .eq('key_prefix', prefix)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async revoke(userId: string, id: string): Promise<void> {
    const { error } = await this.db
      .from('api_keys')
      .update({ revoked_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .eq('id', id);
    if (error) throw error;
  }

  async touch(prefix: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.db
      .from('api_keys')
      .update({ last_used_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('key_prefix', prefix);
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database, Tables, TablesInsert } from '@/types/database';

type Client = SupabaseClient<Database>;

export class AuditRepository {
  constructor(private readonly db: Client) {}

  async log(input: TablesInsert<'audit_logs'>): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.db.from('audit_logs').insert(input);
    } catch {
      // Audit failures must never break the request.
    }
  }

  async list(params: { page: number; pageSize: number; action?: string; userId?: string }) {
    const from = (params.page - 1) * params.pageSize;
    const to = from + params.pageSize - 1;
    let q = this.db
      .from('audit_logs')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact' })
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .range(from, to);
    if (params.action) q = q.eq('action', params.action);
    if (params.userId) q = q.eq('user_id', params.userId);
    const { data, error, count } = await q;
    if (error) throw error;
    return { items: (data ?? []) as Tables<'audit_logs'>[], total: count ?? 0 };
  }
}
```

```ts
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';

export async function requireUser() {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) redirect('/login');
  return { user, supabase };
}
```

### `src/server/guards/require-admin.ts`
```ts
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { isAdmin } from '@/lib/security/permissions';

export async function requireAdmin() {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) redirect('/login?next=/admin');

  const { data: profile } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('role')
    .eq('id', user.id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (!profile || !isAdmin(profile.role)) redirect('/dashboard');
  return { user, profile, supabase };
}
```

### `src/server/services/conversation.service.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database, Tables } from '@/types/database';
import { ConversationsRepository } from '@/server/repositories/conversations.repo';
import type { CreateConversationInput, UpdateConversationInput } from '@/lib/validation/chat';

export class ConversationService {
  private readonly repo: ConversationsRepository;

  constructor(db: SupabaseClient<Database>) {
    this.repo = new ConversationsRepository(db);
  }

  async getOwnedOrThrow(conversationId: string, userId: string): Promise<Tables<'conversations'>> {
    const conv = await this.repo.findById(conversationId);
    if (!conv || conv.user_id !== userId) {
      const err = new Error('المحادثة غير موجودة') as Error & { code: string; status: number };
      err.code = 'not_found';
      err.status = 404;
      throw err;
    }
    return conv;
  }

  async list(params: {
    userId: string;
    page?: number;
    pageSize?: number;
    includeArchived?: boolean;
    onlyArchived?: boolean;
    onlyFavorites?: boolean;
    search?: string;
  }) {
    return this.repo.list({
      userId: params.userId,
      page: params.page ?? 1,
      pageSize: params.pageSize ?? 20,
      includeArchived: params.includeArchived,
      onlyArchived: params.onlyArchived,
      onlyFavorites: params.onlyFavorites,
      search: params.search,
    });
  }

  async create(userId: string, input: CreateConversationInput): Promise<Tables<'conversations'>> {
    return this.repo.create({
      user_id: userId,
      title: input.title ?? 'محادثة جديدة',
      model: input.model,
      system_prompt: input.systemPrompt ?? null,
    });
  }

  async update(
    userId: string,
    conversationId: string,
    input: UpdateConversationInput,
  ): Promise<Tables<'conversations'>> {
    await this.getOwnedOrThrow(conversationId, userId);
    const patch: Partial<Tables<'conversations'>> = {};
    if (input.title !== undefined) patch.title = input.title;
    if (input.isArchived !== undefined) patch.is_archived = input.isArchived;
    if (input.isFavorite !== undefined) patch.is_favorite = input.isFavorite;
    if (input.systemPrompt !== undefined) patch.system_prompt = input.systemPrompt;
    return this.repo.update(conversationId, patch);
  }

  async delete(userId: string, conversationId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.getOwnedOrThrow(conversationId, userId);
    await this.repo.delete(conversationId);
  }

  async autoTitle(conversationId: string, firstUserMessage: string): Promise<void> {
    const conv = await this.repo.findById(conversationId);
    if (!conv || conv.title !== 'محادثة جديدة') return;
    const title = firstUserMessage.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 60);
    if (title) await this.repo.update(conversationId, { title });
  }
}
```

### `src/server/services/usage.service.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database, Tables } from '@/types/database';
import { UsageRepository } from '@/server/repositories/usage.repo';
import { ProfilesRepository } from '@/server/repositories/profiles.repo';
import { FREE_TIER } from '@/config/constants';

export class UsageService {
  private readonly repo: UsageRepository;
  private readonly profiles: ProfilesRepository;

  constructor(db: SupabaseClient<Database>) {
    this.repo = new UsageRepository(db);
    this.profiles = new ProfilesRepository(db);
  }

  async record(input: {
    userId: string;
    conversationId?: string | null;
    model: string;
    provider: string;
    promptTokens: number;
    completionTokens: number;
    totalTokens: number;
    durationMs: number;
    status?: string;
  }) {
    return this.repo.create({
      user_id: input.userId,
      conversation_id: input.conversationId ?? null,
      model: input.model,
      provider: input.provider,
      prompt_tokens: input.promptTokens,
      completion_tokens: input.completionTokens,
      total_tokens: input.totalTokens,
      request_duration_ms: input.durationMs,
      request_status: input.status ?? 'success',
    });
  }

  async checkQuota(userId: string): Promise<{
    allowed: boolean;
    used: { messages: number; tokens: number };
    limits: { messages: number; tokens: number };
    remaining: { messages: number; tokens: number };
  }> {
    const profile = await this.profiles.findById(userId);
    const messageLimit = profile?.daily_message_limit ?? FREE_TIER.dailyMessages;
    const tokenLimit = profile?.daily_token_limit ?? FREE_TIER.dailyTokens;

    const totals = await this.repo.todayTotals(userId);
    const remainingMessages = Math.max(messageLimit - totals.messages, 0);
    const remainingTokens = Math.max(tokenLimit - totals.totalTokens, 0);

    return {
      allowed: remainingMessages > 0 && remainingTokens > 0,
      used: { messages: totals.messages, tokens: totals.totalTokens },
      limits: { messages: messageLimit, tokens: tokenLimit },
      remaining: { messages: remainingMessages, tokens: remainingTokens },
    };
  }

  async userDaily(userId: string, days = 30) {
    return this.repo.userDaily(userId, days);
  }

  async platformDaily(days = 30) {
    return this.repo.platformDaily(days);
  }
}
```

### `src/server/services/api-key.service.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database } from '@/types/database';
import { ApiKeysRepository } from '@/server/repositories/api-keys.repo';
import { generateApiKey, safeEqual, sha256 } from '@/lib/security/hash';
import type { CreateApiKeyInput } from '@/lib/validation/api-keys';

export class ApiKeyService {
  private readonly repo: ApiKeysRepository;

  constructor(db: SupabaseClient<Database>) {
    this.repo = new ApiKeysRepository(db);
  }

  list(userId: string) {
    return this.repo.listForUser(userId);
  }

  async create(userId: string, input: CreateApiKeyInput) {
    const key = generateApiKey();
    const expiresAt = input.expiresInDays
      ? new Date(Date.now() + input.expiresInDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()
      : null;

    await this.repo.create({
      user_id: userId,
      name: input.name,
      key_prefix: key.prefix,
      key_hash: key.hash,
      scopes: input.scopes,
      expires_at: expiresAt,
    });

    // The full key is returned ONLY here — it is never stored in plaintext.
    return { fullKey: key.full, prefix: key.prefix };
  }

  async revoke(userId: string, id: string) {
    return this.repo.revoke(userId, id);
  }

  async verify(rawKey: string): Promise<{ userId: string; scopes: string[] } | null> {
    const parts = rawKey.split('_');
    if (parts.length < 3) return null;
    const prefix = `${parts[0]}_${parts[1]}`;
    const record = await this.repo.findByPrefix(prefix);
    if (!record || record.revoked_at) return null;
    if (record.expires_at && new Date(record.expires_at) < new Date()) return null;

    const hash = sha256(rawKey);
    if (!safeEqual(hash, record.key_hash)) return null;

    await this.repo.touch(prefix);
    return { userId: record.user_id, scopes: record.scopes };
  }
}
```

### `src/server/services/chat.service.ts`
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database } from '@/types/database';
import { getProviderForModel } from '@/lib/ai/factory';
import { ProviderError } from '@/lib/ai/provider';
import type { AIMessage } from '@/types/ai';
import { ConversationService } from './conversation.service';
import { MessageService } from './message.service';
import { UsageService } from './usage.service';
import { AiModelsRepository } from '@/server/repositories/ai-models.repo';

const DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT =
  'أنت مساعد ذكي عربي تُدعى Nados. أجب بدقة ووضوح وباللغة العربية الفصحى المبسطة ما لم يُطلب غير ذلك. كن موجزًا ومفيدًا.';

export class ChatService {
  private readonly conversations: ConversationService;
  private readonly messages: MessageService;
  private readonly usage: UsageService;
  private readonly models: AiModelsRepository;

  constructor(private readonly db: SupabaseClient<Database>) {
    this.conversations = new ConversationService(db);
    this.messages = new MessageService(db);
    this.usage = new UsageService(db);
    this.models = new AiModelsRepository(db);
  }

  async prepareChat(params: {
    userId: string;
    conversationId?: string;
    model: string;
    userContent: string;
    systemPrompt?: string;
    clientMessageId?: string;
  }) {
    const model = await this.models.findBySlug(params.model);
    if (!model || !model.is_enabled) {
      throw this.error('النموذج غير متاح', 'model_not_available', 400);
    }

    const quota = await this.usage.checkQuota(params.userId);
    if (!quota.allowed) {
      throw this.error('تجاوزت الحد اليومي المجاني', 'quota_exceeded', 429);
    }

    // Get or create conversation
    let conversationId = params.conversationId;
    if (conversationId) {
      await this.conversations.getOwnedOrThrow(conversationId, params.userId);
    } else {
      const created = await this.conversations.create(params.userId, {
        model: params.model,
        systemPrompt: params.systemPrompt,
      });
      conversationId = created.id;
    }

    // Save user message
    const userMessage = await this.messages.append({
      userId: params.userId,
      conversationId,
      role: 'user',
      content: params.userContent,
      clientMessageId: params.clientMessageId,
    });

    // Auto-title on first message
    const { total } = await this.messages.list(params.userId, conversationId);
    if (total === 1) {
      await this.conversations.autoTitle(conversationId, params.userContent);
    }

    // Build message history
    const { items } = await this.messages.list(params.userId, conversationId);
    const conversation = await this.conversations.getOwnedOrThrow(conversationId, params.userId);
    const systemPrompt = conversation.system_prompt ?? params.systemPrompt ?? DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT;

    const history: AIMessage[] = [
      { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
      ...items
        .filter((m) => m.role !== 'system' && m.status === 'complete')
        .map((m) => ({ role: m.role as AIMessage['role'], content: m.content })),
    ];

    return {
      conversationId,
      userMessage,
      model: params.model,
      provider: model.provider,
      history,
    };
  }

  async *stream(params: {
    userId: string;
    conversationId: string;
    model: string;
    provider: string;
    history: AIMessage[];
    signal?: AbortSignal;
  }): AsyncIterable<{ delta: string; done: boolean; full: string }> {
    const provider = getProviderForModel(params.model);
    const startedAt = Date.now();
    let full = '';

    try {
      for await (const chunk of provider.streamResponse({
        model: params.model,
        messages: params.history,
        signal: params.signal,
      })) {
        if (chunk.delta) full += chunk.delta;
        if (chunk.done) {
          const promptTokens = await provider.countTokens(
            params.history.map((m) => m.content).join('\n'),
          );
          const completionTokens = await provider.countTokens(full);
          const totalTokens = promptTokens + completionTokens;

          const assistantMessage = await this.messages.append({
            userId: params.userId,
            conversationId: params.conversationId,
            role: 'assistant',
            content: full,
            model: params.model,
            promptTokens,
            completionTokens,
            totalTokens,
          });

          await this.usage.record({
            userId: params.userId,
            conversationId: params.conversationId,
            model: params.model,
            provider: params.provider,
            promptTokens,
            completionTokens,
            totalTokens,
            durationMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
          });

          yield { delta: '', done: true, full };
          void assistantMessage;
          return;
        }
        yield { delta: chunk.delta, done: false, full };
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ProviderError) {
        await this.usage.record({
          userId: params.userId,
          conversationId: params.conversationId,
          model: params.model,
          provider: params.provider,
          promptTokens: 0,
          completionTokens: 0,
          totalTokens: 0,
          durationMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
          status: 'failed',
        });
        throw err;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  private error(message: string, code: string, status: number) {
    const e = new Error(message) as Error & { code: string; status: number };
    e.code = code;
    e.status = status;
    return e;
  }
}
```

**📌 سيتم إكمال الحزمة B و C في الرسالة التالية مباشرة. جاري المتابعة تلقائيًا...**
# 🅱️ الحزمة B — API Routes وصفحات المصادقة والتسويق

## 📁 API Routes

### `src/app/api/health/route.ts`
```ts
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
export const runtime = 'nodejs';

export async function GET() {
  const started = Date.now();
  let db = 'down';
  try {
    const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
    const { error } = await supabase.from('ai_models').select('id').limit(1);
    if (!error) db = 'up';
  } catch {
    db = 'down';
  }

  return NextResponse.json(
    {
      status: db === 'up' ? 'ok' : 'degraded',
      service: 'nados-ai',
      version: process.env.npm_package_version ?? '0.1.0',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      checks: { db, durationMs: Date.now() - started },
    },
    { status: 200 },
  );
}
```

### `src/app/api/chat/route.ts`
```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { ChatService } from '@/server/services/chat.service';
import { AuditService } from '@/server/services/audit.service';
import { sendMessageSchema } from '@/lib/validation/chat';
import { rateLimit, RATE_LIMITS } from '@/lib/security/rate-limit';
import { logger } from '@/lib/logger';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'يجب تسجيل الدخول' } },
      { status: 401 },
    );
  }

  const rl = rateLimit({ key: `chat:${user.id}`, ...RATE_LIMITS.chat });
  if (!rl.allowed) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, error: { code: 'rate_limited', message: 'عدد الطلبات كبير جدًا' } },
      { status: 429, headers: { 'Retry-After': String(Math.ceil((rl.resetAt - Date.now()) / 1000)) } },
    );
  }

  const raw = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  const parsed = sendMessageSchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: false,
        error: { code: 'invalid_input', message: 'بيانات غير صالحة', details: parsed.error.flatten() },
      },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const audit = new AuditService(supabase);
  const chat = new ChatService(supabase);
  const abort = new AbortController();
  req.signal.addEventListener('abort', () => abort.abort());

  try {
    const prepared = await chat.prepareChat({
      userId: user.id,
      conversationId: parsed.data.conversationId,
      model: parsed.data.model,
      userContent: parsed.data.content,
      systemPrompt: parsed.data.systemPrompt,
      clientMessageId: parsed.data.clientMessageId,
    });

    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
      async start(controller) {
        const send = (event: string, data: unknown) => {
          controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`event: ${event}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`));
        };

        try {
          send('meta', {
            conversationId: prepared.conversationId,
            userMessageId: prepared.userMessage.id,
            model: prepared.model,
          });

          for await (const chunk of chat.stream({
            userId: user.id,
            conversationId: prepared.conversationId,
            model: prepared.model,
            provider: prepared.provider,
            history: prepared.history,
            signal: abort.signal,
          })) {
            if (chunk.delta) send('delta', { text: chunk.delta });
            if (chunk.done) {
              send('done', { conversationId: prepared.conversationId });
              break;
            }
          }
          controller.close();

          void audit.log({
            userId: user.id,
            action: 'chat.send',
            entityType: 'conversation',
            entityId: prepared.conversationId,
            ip: req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0]?.trim() ?? null,
            userAgent: req.headers.get('user-agent'),
            metadata: { model: prepared.model },
          });
        } catch (err) {
          logger.error('chat stream error', { err: String(err) });
          send('error', { message: 'حدث خطأ أثناء توليد الرد' });
          controller.close();
        }
      },
    });

    return new Response(stream, {
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8',
        'Cache-Control': 'no-cache, no-transform',
        Connection: 'keep-alive',
        'X-Accel-Buffering': 'no',
      },
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const e = err as Error & { code?: string; status?: number };
    logger.warn('chat request failed', { code: e.code, message: e.message });
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, error: { code: e.code ?? 'chat_error', message: e.message } },
      { status: e.status ?? 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

### `src/app/api/conversations/route.ts`
```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { ConversationService } from '@/server/services/conversation.service';
import { createConversationSchema } from '@/lib/validation/chat';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const service = new ConversationService(supabase);
  const result = await service.list({
    userId: user.id,
    page: Number(url.searchParams.get('page') ?? 1),
    pageSize: Math.min(Number(url.searchParams.get('pageSize') ?? 20), 50),
    onlyArchived: url.searchParams.get('archived') === 'true',
    onlyFavorites: url.searchParams.get('favorite') === 'true',
    search: url.searchParams.get('q') ?? undefined,
  });
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: result });
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  const parsed = createConversationSchema.safeParse(await req.json().catch(() => null));
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_input', message: 'بيانات غير صالحة', details: parsed.error.flatten() } },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }
  const service = new ConversationService(supabase);
  const created = await service.create(user.id, parsed.data);
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: created }, { status: 201 });
}
```

### `src/app/api/conversations/[id]/route.ts`
```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { ConversationService } from '@/server/services/conversation.service';
import { MessageService } from '@/server/services/message.service';
import { updateConversationSchema } from '@/lib/validation/chat';
import { idParamSchema } from '@/lib/validation/common';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, ctx: Ctx) {
  const { id } = await ctx.params;
  const parsed = idParamSchema.safeParse({ id });
  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_id', message: 'معرّف غير صالح' } }, { status: 400 });

  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    const conversation = await new ConversationService(supabase).getOwnedOrThrow(id, user.id);
    const messages = await new MessageService(supabase).list(user.id, id);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: { conversation, messages: messages.items } });
  } catch (err) {
    const e = err as Error & { status?: number };
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'not_found', message: e.message } }, { status: e.status ?? 500 });
  }
}

export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, ctx: Ctx) {
  const { id } = await ctx.params;
  const parsed = idParamSchema.safeParse({ id });
  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_id', message: 'معرّف غير صالح' } }, { status: 400 });

  const body = updateConversationSchema.safeParse(await req.json().catch(() => null));
  if (!body.success) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_input', message: 'بيانات غير صالحة', details: body.error.flatten() } }, { status: 400 });

  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    const updated = await new ConversationService(supabase).update(user.id, id, body.data);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: updated });
  } catch (err) {
    const e = err as Error & { status?: number };
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'update_failed', message: e.message } }, { status: e.status ?? 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(_req: NextRequest, ctx: Ctx) {
  const { id } = await ctx.params;
  const parsed = idParamSchema.safeParse({ id });
  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_id', message: 'معرّف غير صالح' } }, { status: 400 });

  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    await new ConversationService(supabase).delete(user.id, id);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: null });
  } catch (err) {
    const e = err as Error & { status?: number };
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'delete_failed', message: e.message } }, { status: e.status ?? 500 });
  }
}
```

### `src/app/api/search/route.ts`
```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { MessageService } from '@/server/services/message.service';
import { searchSchema } from '@/lib/validation/chat';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const parsed = searchSchema.safeParse(Object.fromEntries(url.searchParams));
  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_input', message: 'استعلام غير صالح' } }, { status: 400 });

  const result = await new MessageService(supabase).search({
    userId: user.id,
    query: parsed.data.q,
    page: parsed.data.page,
    pageSize: parsed.data.pageSize,
    model: parsed.data.model,
    from: parsed.data.from,
    to: parsed.data.to,
  });
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: result });
}
```

### `src/app/api/notifications/route.ts`
```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { NotificationService } from '@/server/services/notification.service';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const service = new NotificationService(supabase);
  const result = await service.list(user.id, {
    page: Number(url.searchParams.get('page') ?? 1),
    pageSize: Number(url.searchParams.get('pageSize') ?? 20),
    unreadOnly: url.searchParams.get('unread') === 'true',
  });
  const unread = await service.unreadCount(user.id);
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: { ...result, unread } });
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  const body = (await req.json().catch(() => null)) as { ids?: string[]; all?: boolean } | null;
  const service = new NotificationService(supabase);
  if (body?.all) await service.markAllRead(user.id);
  else if (body?.ids?.length) await service.markRead(user.id, body.ids);
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: null });
}
```

```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { ApiKeyService } from '@/server/services/api-key.service';
import { AuditService } from '@/server/services/audit.service';
import { createApiKeySchema } from '@/lib/validation/api-keys';
import { rateLimit, RATE_LIMITS } from '@/lib/security/rate-limit';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

export async function GET() {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });
  const keys = await new ApiKeyService(supabase).list(user.id);
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: keys });
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  const rl = rateLimit({ key: `apikey:${user.id}`, ...RATE_LIMITS.apiKeysCreate });
  if (!rl.allowed) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'rate_limited', message: 'حاول لاحقًا' } }, { status: 429 });

  const parsed = createApiKeySchema.safeParse(await req.json().catch(() => null));
  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_input', message: 'بيانات غير صالحة', details: parsed.error.flatten() } }, { status: 400 });

  const result = await new ApiKeyService(supabase).create(user.id, parsed.data);
  await new AuditService(supabase).log({
    userId: user.id,
    action: 'api_key.create',
    entityType: 'api_key',
    entityId: null,
    metadata: { name: parsed.data.name, scopes: parsed.data.scopes },
  });
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: result }, { status: 201 });
}
```

### `src/app/api/api-keys/[id]/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { ApiKeyService } from '@/server/services/api-key.service';
import { AuditService } from '@/server/services/audit.service';
import { idParamSchema } from '@/lib/validation/common';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export async function DELETE(_req: NextRequest, ctx: Ctx) {
  const { id } = await ctx.params;
  const parsed = idParamSchema.safeParse({ id });
  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_id', message: 'معرّف غير صالح' } }, { status: 400 });

  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'غير مسجل' } }, { status: 401 });

  await new ApiKeyService(supabase).revoke(user.id, id);
  await new AuditService(supabase).log({ userId: user.id, action: 'api_key.revoke', entityType: 'api_key', entityId: id });
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: null });
}
```

```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { ContactRepository } from '@/server/repositories/contact.repo';
import { contactSchema } from '@/lib/validation/contact';
import { rateLimit, RATE_LIMITS } from '@/lib/security/rate-limit';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0]?.trim() ?? 'anon';
  const rl = rateLimit({ key: `contact:${ip}`, ...RATE_LIMITS.contact });
  if (!rl.allowed) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'rate_limited', message: 'حاول لاحقًا' } }, { status: 429 });

  const parsed = contactSchema.safeParse(await req.json().catch(() => null));
  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_input', message: 'بيانات غير صالحة', details: parsed.error.flatten() } }, { status: 400 });

  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const repo = new ContactRepository(supabase);
  await repo.create({ ...parsed.data, user_id: user?.id ?? null });
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: null }, { status: 201 });
}
```

### `src/app/api/v1/chat/completions/route.ts`
```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createAdminSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { ApiKeyService } from '@/server/services/api-key.service';
import { ChatService } from '@/server/services/chat.service';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/security/rate-limit';
import { sendMessageSchema } from '@/lib/validation/chat';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

/** Public developer API: POST /api/v1/chat/completions */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const auth = req.headers.get('authorization') ?? '';
  const raw = auth.startsWith('Bearer ') ? auth.slice(7).trim() : '';
  if (!raw) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'مفتاح API مطلوب' } },
      { status: 401 },
    );
  }

  const admin = await createAdminSupabaseClient();
  const keyService = new ApiKeyService(admin);
  const verified = await keyService.verify(raw);
  if (!verified) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_key', message: 'مفتاح غير صالح' } }, { status: 401 });
  }
  if (!verified.scopes.includes('chat:write')) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'forbidden', message: 'لا تملك صلاحية' } }, { status: 403 });
  }

  const rl = rateLimit({ key: `apiv1:${verified.userId}`, limit: 60, windowMs: 60_000 });
  if (!rl.allowed) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'rate_limited', message: 'تجاوزت الحد' } }, { status: 429 });

  const parsed = sendMessageSchema.safeParse(await req.json().catch(() => null));
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'invalid_input', message: 'بيانات غير صالحة', details: parsed.error.flatten() } }, { status: 400 });
  }

  try {
    const chat = new ChatService(admin);
    const prepared = await chat.prepareChat({
      userId: verified.userId,
      conversationId: parsed.data.conversationId,
      model: parsed.data.model,
      userContent: parsed.data.content,
      systemPrompt: parsed.data.systemPrompt,
    });

    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
      async start(controller) {
        const send = (event: string, data: unknown) =>
          controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`event: ${event}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`));
        try {
          send('meta', { conversationId: prepared.conversationId });
          for await (const chunk of chat.stream({
            userId: verified.userId,
            conversationId: prepared.conversationId,
            model: prepared.model,
            provider: prepared.provider,
            history: prepared.history,
          })) {
            if (chunk.delta) send('delta', { text: chunk.delta });
            if (chunk.done) { send('done', {}); break; }
          }
          controller.close();
        } catch {
          send('error', { message: 'حدث خطأ' });
          controller.close();
        }
      },
    });

    return new Response(stream, {
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8',
        'Cache-Control': 'no-cache, no-transform',
      },
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const e = err as Error & { code?: string; status?: number };
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: e.code ?? 'error', message: e.message } }, { status: e.status ?? 500 });
  }
}
```

### `src/app/(auth)/logout/route.ts`
```ts
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';

export async function POST() {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  await supabase.auth.signOut();
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
}

export async function GET() {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  await supabase.auth.signOut();
  return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/', process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL ?? 'http://localhost:3000'));
}
```

### `src/app/(auth)/layout.tsx`
```tsx
import Link from 'next/link';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Logo } from '@/components/shared/logo';

export default function AuthLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid min-h-dvh lg:grid-cols-2">
      <aside className="relative hidden overflow-hidden border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface)] lg:flex lg:flex-col lg:justify-between lg:border-l rtl:border-l ltr:border-r p-12">
        <div className="grid-noise absolute inset-0 opacity-70" aria-hidden="true" />
        <div className="relative">
          <Logo size="lg" />
        </div>
        <div className="relative max-w-md space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-semibold leading-tight">
            محادثات ذكية بـ
            <span className="gradient-text"> العربية</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-[color:var(--muted)]">
            انضم إلى Nados AI واستفد من محادثات ذكية، بحث متقدم، وإدارة كاملة لكل محادثاتك — كل
            ذلك مجانًا.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="relative text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          © {new Date().getFullYear()} Nados AI
        </div>
      </aside>

      <main className="flex min-h-dvh items-center justify-center px-4 py-10 sm:px-8">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md">
          <div className="mb-8 flex justify-center lg:hidden">
            <Logo size="lg" />
          </div>

          <p className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
            <Link href="/" className="hover:text-[color:var(--foreground)]">
              ← العودة إلى الرئيسية
            </Link>
          </p>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
```
