### `src/components/auth/login-form.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Loader2, Mail, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { loginSchema, type LoginInput } from '@/lib/validation/auth';

export function LoginForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<LoginInput>({ resolver: zodResolver(loginSchema), defaultValues: { email: '', password: '' } });

  async function onSubmit(data: LoginInput) {
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email: data.email,
        password: data.password,
      });
      if (error) {
        toast.error('فشل تسجيل الدخول', { description: 'تأكد من البريد وكلمة المرور.' });
        return;
      }
      const next = params.get('next') ?? '/dashboard';
      router.replace(next);
      router.refresh();
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="email">البريد الإلكتروني</Label>
        <div className="relative">
          <Mail className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-[color:var(--muted)] ltr:left-3 rtl:right-3" />
          <Input
            id="email"
            type="email"
            autoComplete="email"
            placeholder="you@example.com"
            className="ltr:pl-9 rtl:pr-9"
            aria-invalid={!!errors.email}
            {...register('email')}
          />
        </div>
        {errors.email && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.email.message}</p>}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <Label htmlFor="password">كلمة المرور</Label>
          <Link href="/forgot-password" className="text-xs text-[color:var(--accent)] hover:underline">
            نسيت كلمة المرور؟
          </Link>
        </div>
        <div className="relative">
          <Lock className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-[color:var(--muted)] ltr:left-3 rtl:right-3" />
          <Input
            id="password"
            type="password"
            autoComplete="current-password"
            placeholder="••••••••"
            className="ltr:pl-9 rtl:pr-9"
            aria-invalid={!!errors.password}
            {...register('password')}
          />
        </div>
        {errors.password && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.password.message}</p>}
      </div>

      <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={submitting}>
        {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : null}
        {submitting ? 'جارٍ الدخول…' : 'تسجيل الدخول'}
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/auth/register-form.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { registerSchema, type RegisterInput } from '@/lib/validation/auth';

export function RegisterForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<RegisterInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(registerSchema),
    defaultValues: { fullName: '', email: '', username: '', password: '' },
  });

  async function onSubmit(data: RegisterInput) {
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
        email: data.email,
        password: data.password,
        options: {
          data: { full_name: data.fullName, username: data.username },
          emailRedirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/verify-email`,
        },
      });

      if (error) {
        const msg = error.message.includes('already') ? 'البريد مستخدم بالفعل' : 'تعذّر إنشاء الحساب';
        toast.error(msg);
        return;
      }

      toast.success('تم إنشاء حسابك', { description: 'تحقق من بريدك الإلكتروني لتفعيل الحساب.' });
      router.replace('/verify-email');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="fullName">الاسم الكامل</Label>
        <Input id="fullName" autoComplete="name" placeholder="محمد أحمد" {...register('fullName')} aria-invalid={!!errors.fullName} />
        {errors.fullName && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.fullName.message}</p>}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="email">البريد الإلكتروني</Label>
        <Input id="email" type="email" autoComplete="email" placeholder="you@example.com" {...register('email')} aria-invalid={!!errors.email} />
        {errors.email && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.email.message}</p>}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="username">اسم المستخدم</Label>
        <Input id="username" autoComplete="username" placeholder="mohammed" {...register('username')} aria-invalid={!!errors.username} />
        {errors.username && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.username.message}</p>}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="password">كلمة المرور</Label>
        <Input id="password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" placeholder="8 أحرف على الأقل" {...register('password')} aria-invalid={!!errors.password} />
        {errors.password && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.password.message}</p>}
      </div>

      <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={submitting}>
        {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : null}
        {submitting ? 'جارٍ الإنشاء…' : 'إنشاء الحساب'}
      </Button>

      <p className="text-center text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
        بإنشاء الحساب، أنت توافق على{' '}
        <a href="/terms" className="text-[color:var(--accent)] hover:underline">شروط الاستخدام</a>
        {' '}و{' '}
        <a href="/privacy" className="text-[color:var(--accent)] hover:underline">سياسة الخصوصية</a>.
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/auth/forgot-password-form.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Loader2, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { forgotPasswordSchema, type ForgotPasswordInput } from '@/lib/validation/auth';

export function ForgotPasswordForm() {
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);

  const { register, handleSubmit, formState: { errors } } = useForm<ForgotPasswordInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(forgotPasswordSchema),
    defaultValues: { email: '' },
  });

  async function onSubmit(data: ForgotPasswordInput) {
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(data.email, {
        redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/reset-password`,
      });
      setSent(true);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (sent) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)] p-6 text-center">
        <CheckCircle2 className="mx-auto mb-3 h-10 w-10 text-[color:var(--success)]" />
        <h2 className="mb-1 font-medium">تم الإرسال</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          إذا كان البريد مسجلًا لدينا، فستصلك رسالة تحتوي على رابط إعادة التعيين خلال دقائق.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="email">البريد الإلكتروني</Label>
        <Input id="email" type="email" autoComplete="email" placeholder="you@example.com" {...register('email')} aria-invalid={!!errors.email} />
        {errors.email && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.email.message}</p>}
      </div>
      <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={submitting}>
        {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : null}
        إرسال رابط الاستعادة
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/auth/reset-password-form.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { resetPasswordSchema, type ResetPasswordInput } from '@/lib/validation/auth';

export function ResetPasswordForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const { register, handleSubmit, formState: { errors } } = useForm<ResetPasswordInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(resetPasswordSchema),
    defaultValues: { password: '', confirmPassword: '' },
  });

  async function onSubmit(data: ResetPasswordInput) {
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: data.password });
      if (error) {
        toast.error('تعذّر تحديث كلمة المرور', { description: 'قد يكون الرابط منتهي الصلاحية.' });
        return;
      }
      toast.success('تم تحديث كلمة المرور');
      router.replace('/login');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="password">كلمة المرور الجديدة</Label>
        <Input id="password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" {...register('password')} aria-invalid={!!errors.password} />
        {errors.password && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.password.message}</p>}
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="confirmPassword">تأكيد كلمة المرور</Label>
        <Input id="confirmPassword" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" {...register('confirmPassword')} aria-invalid={!!errors.confirmPassword} />
        {errors.confirmPassword && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.confirmPassword.message}</p>}
      </div>
      <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={submitting}>
        {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : null}
        تحديث كلمة المرور
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

### `src/app/(marketing)/services/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { siteConfig } from '@/config/site';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { MessageSquare, Search, Library, KeyRound, BarChart3, Bell } from 'lucide-react';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'الخدمات',
  description: 'اكتشف خدمات Nados AI: محادثة ذكية، بحث، مكتبة، API للمطورين.',
  alternates: { canonical: `${siteConfig.url}/services` },
};

const services = [
  { icon: MessageSquare, title: 'محادثة ذكية', description: 'حاور أفضل نماذج الذكاء الاصطناعي ببث فوري وواجهة عربية سلسة.' },
  { icon: Search, title: 'بحث داخلي', description: 'ابحث في كل محادثاتك بكلمات مفتاحية مع فلاتر متقدمة.' },
  { icon: Library, title: 'المكتبة', description: 'احفظ النتائج المهمة ونظّمها للرجوع إليها لاحقًا.' },
  { icon: KeyRound, title: 'API للمطورين', description: 'مفاتيح API آمنة مبنية للاستخدام في تطبيقاتك.' },

  { icon: Bell, title: 'إشعارات ذكية', description: 'تنبيهات عند الوصول لحدود الاستهلاك أو الأحداث المهمة.' },
```

### `src/components/marketing/contact-form.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Loader2, Send, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { contactSchema, type ContactInput } from '@/lib/validation/contact';

export function ContactForm() {
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);

  const { register, handleSubmit, formState: { errors }, reset } = useForm<ContactInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(contactSchema),
    defaultValues: { name: '', email: '', subject: '', message: '' },
  });

  async function onSubmit(data: ContactInput) {
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/contact', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
      });
      const json = await res.json();
      if (!json.success) {
        toast.error('تعذّر الإرسال', { description: json.error?.message });
        return;
      }
      setSent(true);
      reset();
    } catch {
      toast.error('حدث خطأ أثناء الإرسال');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (sent) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)] p-8 text-center">
        <CheckCircle2 className="mx-auto mb-3 h-12 w-12 text-[color:var(--success)]" />
        <h2 className="mb-2 text-lg font-medium">تم استلام رسالتك</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">سنرد عليك في أقرب وقت ممكن.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-5 rounded-lg border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface)] p-6 sm:p-8" noValidate>
      <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="name">الاسم</Label>
          <Input id="name" autoComplete="name" {...register('name')} aria-invalid={!!errors.name} />
          {errors.name && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.name.message}</p>}
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="email">البريد الإلكتروني</Label>
          <Input id="email" type="email" autoComplete="email" {...register('email')} aria-invalid={!!errors.email} />
          {errors.email && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.email.message}</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="subject">الموضوع</Label>
        <Input id="subject" {...register('subject')} aria-invalid={!!errors.subject} />
        {errors.subject && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.subject.message}</p>}
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="message">الرسالة</Label>
        <Textarea id="message" rows={6} {...register('message')} aria-invalid={!!errors.message} />
        {errors.message && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.message.message}</p>}
      </div>
      <Button type="submit" disabled={submitting} className="w-full gap-2">
        {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Send className="h-4 w-4" />}
        إرسال الرسالة
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/marketing/faq-accordion.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';

type FaqItem = { q: string; a: string };

export function FaqAccordion({ items }: { items: FaqItem[] }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<number | null>(0);

  return (
    <div className="divide-y divide-[color:var(--border)] rounded-lg border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface)]">
      {items.map((item, i) => {
        const isOpen = open === i;
        return (
          <div key={i}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOpen(isOpen ? null : i)}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-4 text-start font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)]"
              aria-expanded={isOpen}
            >
              <span>{item.q}</span>
              <ChevronDown className={cn('h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform', isOpen && 'rotate-180')} />
            </button>
            {isOpen && (
              <div className="px-5 pb-5 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                {item.a}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
import type { MetadataRoute } from 'next';
import { siteConfig } from '@/config/site';

export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  return {
    rules: [
      {
        userAgent: '*',
        allow: '/',
        disallow: [
          '/dashboard/',
          '/chat/',
          '/settings/',
          '/admin/',
          '/api/',
          '/library',
          '/notifications',
          '/usage',
          '/api-keys',
          '/search',
        ],
      },
    ],
    sitemap: `${siteConfig.url}/sitemap.xml`,
    host: siteConfig.url,
  };
}
```

### `src/app/sitemap.ts`
```ts
import type { MetadataRoute } from 'next';
import { siteConfig } from '@/config/site';

export default function sitemap(): MetadataRoute.Sitemap {
  const now = new Date();
  const routes = ['', '/about', '/services', '/contact', '/faq', '/privacy', '/terms'];

  return routes.map((route) => ({
    url: `${siteConfig.url}${route}`,
    lastModified: now,
    changeFrequency: route === '' ? 'daily' : 'monthly',
    priority: route === '' ? 1 : 0.7,
  }));
}
```

---

**📌 جاري كتابة الحزمة C (المحادثة + Streaming + لوحة التحكم + الإعدادات + الإدارة)...**
# 🅲 الحزمة C — المحادثة، Streaming، لوحة التحكم، الإعدادات، والإدارة

## 📁 محادثة + Streaming

### `src/features/chat/store.ts`
```ts
'use client';

import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { AIMessage, AIStreamChunk } from '@/types/ai';

export type LocalMessage = {
  id: string;
  role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system' | 'tool';
  content: string;
  status: 'pending' | 'streaming' | 'complete' | 'failed';
  createdAt: string;
  clientId?: string;
};

type ChatState = {
  messages: LocalMessage[];
  streaming: boolean;
  abortController: AbortController | null;
  activeConversationId: string | null;
  setActiveConversation: (id: string | null) => void;
  setMessages: (msgs: LocalMessage[]) => void;
  appendMessage: (msg: LocalMessage) => void;
  updateMessage: (id: string, patch: Partial<LocalMessage>) => void;
  appendDelta: (id: string, delta: string) => void;
  setStreaming: (v: boolean) => void;
  setAbortController: (ac: AbortController | null) => void;
  reset: () => void;
};

export const useChatStore = create<ChatState>((set) => ({
  messages: [],
  streaming: false,
  abortController: null,
  activeConversationId: null,
  setActiveConversation: (id) => set({ activeConversationId: id }),
  setMessages: (msgs) => set({ messages: msgs }),
  appendMessage: (msg) => set((s) => ({ messages: [...s.messages, msg] })),
  updateMessage: (id, patch) =>
    set((s) => ({ messages: s.messages.map((m) => (m.id === id ? { ...m, ...patch } : m)) })),
  appendDelta: (id, delta) =>
    set((s) => ({
      messages: s.messages.map((m) => (m.id === id ? { ...m, content: m.content + delta } : m)),
    })),
  setStreaming: (v) => set({ streaming: v }),
  setAbortController: (ac) => set({ abortController: ac }),
  reset: () => set({ messages: [], streaming: false, abortController: null, activeConversationId: null }),
}));

export type { AIMessage, AIStreamChunk };
```

### `src/features/chat/hooks/use-chat-stream.ts`
```ts
'use client';

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useChatStore, type LocalMessage } from '../store';

type SendParams = {
  content: string;
  model: string;
  conversationId?: string;
  systemPrompt?: string;
};

function uid(): string {
  return typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && 'randomUUID' in crypto
    ? crypto.randomUUID()
    : Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
}

export function useChatStream() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const {
    appendMessage,
    appendDelta,
    updateMessage,
    setStreaming,
    setAbortController,
    setActiveConversation,
  } = useChatStore();

  const send = useCallback(
    async (params: SendParams) => {
      const userClientId = uid();
      const assistantId = uid();

      const userMessage: LocalMessage = {
        id: userClientId,
        role: 'user',
        content: params.content,
        status: 'complete',
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        clientId: userClientId,
      };
      const assistantMessage: LocalMessage = {
        id: assistantId,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: '',
        status: 'streaming',
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };

      appendMessage(userMessage);
      appendMessage(assistantMessage);

      const abort = new AbortController();
      setAbortController(abort);
      setStreaming(true);

      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/chat', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            content: params.content,
            model: params.model,
            conversationId: params.conversationId,
            systemPrompt: params.systemPrompt,
            clientMessageId: userClientId,
          }),
          signal: abort.signal,
        });

        if (!res.ok || !res.body) {
          const json = await res.json().catch(() => null);
          throw new Error(json?.error?.message ?? 'فشل الطلب');
        }

        const reader = res.body.getReader();
        const decoder = new TextDecoder();
        let buffer = '';
        let newConversationId = params.conversationId ?? null;
        let completed = false;

        while (true) {
          const { value, done } = await reader.read();
          if (done) break;
          buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });

          const events = buffer.split('\n\n');
          buffer = events.pop() ?? '';

          for (const block of events) {
            if (!block.trim()) continue;
            const lines = block.split('\n');
            let event = 'message';
            let data = '';
            for (const line of lines) {
              if (line.startsWith('event:')) event = line.slice(6).trim();
              else if (line.startsWith('data:')) data += line.slice(5).trim();
            }
            if (!data) continue;
            let parsed: Record<string, unknown>;
            try {
              parsed = JSON.parse(data);
            } catch {
              continue;
            }

            if (event === 'meta') {
              const cid = (parsed.conversationId as string) ?? null;
              newConversationId = cid;
              if (cid) setActiveConversation(cid);
            } else if (event === 'delta') {
              const text = (parsed.text as string) ?? '';
              if (text) appendDelta(assistantId, text);
            } else if (event === 'done') {
              completed = true;
              updateMessage(assistantId, { status: 'complete' });
            } else if (event === 'error') {
              const msg = (parsed.message as string) ?? 'خطأ';
              updateMessage(assistantId, { status: 'failed', content: msg });
              toast.error(msg);
            }
          }
        }

        if (!completed && !abort.signal.aborted) {
          updateMessage(assistantId, { status: 'complete' });
        }

        // First message in a new conversation: navigate to /chat/[id]
        if (newConversationId && !params.conversationId) {
          router.replace(`/chat/${newConversationId}`);
        }
        router.refresh();
      } catch (err) {
        if ((err as Error).name !== 'AbortError') {
          updateMessage(assistantId, { status: 'failed' });
          toast.error('تعذّر إرسال الرسالة');
        }
      } finally {
        setStreaming(false);
        setAbortController(null);
      }
    },
    [appendDelta, appendMessage, router, setAbortController, setActiveConversation, setStreaming, updateMessage],
  );

  const stop = useCallback(() => {
    const { abortController } = useChatStore.getState();
    abortController?.abort();
    setStreaming(false);
    setAbortController(null);
  }, [setAbortController, setStreaming]);

  return { send, stop };
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import { ChevronDown, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
```

### `src/components/chat/chat-composer.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import { ArrowUp, Square, Paperclip } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';

type Props = {
  onSubmit: (content: string) => void;
  onStop: () => void;
  streaming: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
};

export function ChatComposer({ onSubmit, onStop, streaming, disabled }: Props) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState('');
  const ref = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null);

  const submit = () => {
    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (!trimmed || streaming || disabled) return;
    onSubmit(trimmed);
    setValue('');
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      if (ref.current) {
        ref.current.style.height = 'auto';
      }
    });
  };

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      submit();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="sticky bottom-0 border-t border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--background)]/95 backdrop-blur">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-3 sm:py-4">
        <div className="relative flex items-end gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface)] p-2 shadow-sm">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" disabled className="shrink-0" aria-label="إرفاق ملف (قريبًا)">
            <Paperclip className="h-4 w-4" />
          </Button>
          <Textarea
            ref={ref}
            value={value}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setValue(e.target.value);
              const el = e.target as HTMLTextAreaElement;
              el.style.height = 'auto';
              el.style.height = `${Math.min(el.scrollHeight, 200)}px`;
            }}
            onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
            placeholder="اكتب رسالتك… (Shift + Enter لسطر جديد)"
            rows={1}
            disabled={disabled}
            className={cn(
              'min-h-[38px] resize-none border-0 bg-transparent px-2 py-2 shadow-none focus-visible:ring-0',
            )}
          />
          {streaming ? (
            <Button
              onClick={onStop}
              size="icon"
              variant="secondary"
              className="shrink-0"
              aria-label="إيقاف التوليد"
            >
              <Square className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <Button
              onClick={submit}
              size="icon"
              className="shrink-0"
              disabled={!value.trim() || disabled}
              aria-label="إرسال"
            >
              <ArrowUp className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-center text-[11px] text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          قد يرتكب الذكاء الاصطناعي أخطاء. تحقق من المعلومات المهمة.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/chat/message-item.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Copy, Check, RefreshCw, ThumbsUp, ThumbsDown, User } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback } from '@/components/ui/avatar';
import { Logo } from '@/components/shared/logo';
import { MarkdownRenderer } from './markdown-renderer';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';
import type { LocalMessage } from '@/features/chat/store';

type Props = {
  message: LocalMessage;
  onRegenerate?: () => void;
};

export function MessageItem({ message, onRegenerate }: Props) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [rating, setRating] = useState<'up' | 'down' | null>(null);
  const isUser = message.role === 'user';

  const copy = async () => {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(message.content);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
  };

  return (
    <article
      className={cn(
        'group flex gap-3 px-4 py-4 sm:gap-4 sm:px-6',
        isUser && 'bg-[color:var(--surface)]/40',
      )}
    >
      <div className="shrink-0 pt-0.5">
        {isUser ? (
          <Avatar className="h-8 w-8">
            <AvatarFallback>
              <User className="h-4 w-4" />
            </AvatarFallback>
          </Avatar>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-[color:var(--accent)] to-[color:var(--accent-blue)]">
            <Logo showText={false} size="sm" href={undefined} />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          <span className="font-medium text-[color:var(--foreground)]">
            {isUser ? 'أنت' : 'Nados AI'}
          </span>
          {message.status === 'streaming' && (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="h-1 w-1 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-[color:var(--accent)]" />
              يكتب…
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="prose-sm max-w-none">
          {isUser ? (
            <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words leading-relaxed">{message.content}</p>
          ) : (
            <MarkdownRenderer content={message.content} />
          )}
        </div>

        {message.status !== 'streaming' && message.content && (
          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1 opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100 focus-within:opacity-100">
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" onClick={copy} aria-label="نسخ">
              {copied ? <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[color:var(--success)]" /> : <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
            </Button>
            {!isUser && onRegenerate && (
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" onClick={onRegenerate} aria-label="إعادة التوليد">
                <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </Button>
            )}
            {!isUser && (
              <>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="icon-sm"
                  onClick={() => setRating('up')}
                  aria-label="إعجاب"
                  className={cn(rating === 'up' && 'text-[color:var(--success)]')}
                >
                  <ThumbsUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="icon-sm"
                  onClick={() => setRating('down')}
                  aria-label="عدم إعجاب"
                  className={cn(rating === 'down' && 'text-[color:var(--danger)]')}
                >
                  <ThumbsDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </Button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/chat/markdown-renderer.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { memo } from 'react';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
import { CodeBlock } from './code-block';

export const MarkdownRenderer = memo(function MarkdownRenderer({ content }: { content: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="prose-ar max-w-none text-[15px] leading-relaxed">
      <ReactMarkdown
        remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}
        components={{
          code(props) {
            const { children, className, ...rest } = props as {
              children?: React.ReactNode;
              className?: string;
            };
            const match = /language-(\w+)/.exec(className ?? '');
            const isInline = !className;
            if (isInline) {
              return (
                <code
                  className="rounded bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)] px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.85em]"
                  {...rest}
                >
                  {children}
                </code>
              );
            }
            return <CodeBlock language={match?.[1]} code={String(children).replace(/\n$/, '')} />;
          },
          a(props) {
            const { href, children } = props;
            return (
              <a
                href={href}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow"
                className="text-[color:var(--accent)] underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
              >
                {children}
              </a>
            );
          },
          p(props) {
            return <p className="my-3 whitespace-pre-wrap">{props.children}</p>;
          },
          ul(props) {
            return <ul className="my-3 list-disc space-y-1 pe-6">{props.children}</ul>;
          },
          ol(props) {
            return <ol className="my-3 list-decimal space-y-1 pe-6">{props.children}</ol>;
          },
          h1(props) {
            return <h1 className="my-4 text-xl font-semibold">{props.children}</h1>;
          },
          h2(props) {
            return <h2 className="my-4 text-lg font-semibold">{props.children}</h2>;
          },
          h3(props) {
            return <h3 className="my-3 text-base font-semibold">{props.children}</h3>;
          },
          blockquote(props) {
            return (
              <blockquote className="my-3 border-s-2 border-[color:var(--accent)]/40 ps-4 text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                {props.children}
              </blockquote>
            );
          },
          table(props) {
            return (
              <div className="my-3 overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">{props.children}</table>
              </div>
            );
          },
          th(props) {
            return <th className="border border-[color:var(--border)] px-3 py-1.5 text-start">{props.children}</th>;
          },
          td(props) {
            return <td className="border border-[color:var(--border)] px-3 py-1.5">{props.children}</td>;
          },
        }}
      >
        {content}
      </ReactMarkdown>
    </div>
  );
});
```

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Check, Copy } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';

type Props = { code: string; language?: string };

export function CodeBlock({ code, language }: Props) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const copy = async () => {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
  };

  return (
    <div className="my-3 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[#0b0c10]">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[color:var(--border)] px-3 py-1.5">
        <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-[color:var(--muted)]">{language ?? 'text'}</span>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" onClick={copy} aria-label="نسخ الكود">
          {copied ? <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[color:var(--success)]" /> : <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <pre dir="ltr" className="overflow-x-auto p-3 text-[13px] leading-relaxed">
        <code className="font-mono text-[color:#e5e7eb]">{code}</code>
      </pre>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/chat/message-list.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { MessageItem } from './message-item';
import type { LocalMessage } from '@/features/chat/store';
import { useChatStore } from '@/features/chat/store';

type Props = {
  onRegenerate?: (index: number) => void;
};

export function MessageList({ onRegenerate }: Props) {
  const messages = useChatStore((s) => s.messages);
  const bottomRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'end' });
  }, [messages.length, messages[messages.length - 1]?.content]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl">
      {messages.map((m, i) => (
        <MessageItem
          key={m.id}
          message={m}
          onRegenerate={m.role === 'assistant' ? () => onRegenerate?.(i) : undefined}
        />
      ))}
      <div ref={bottomRef} className="h-4" />
    </div>
  );
}

export type { LocalMessage };
```

### `src/components/chat/chat-shell.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { ModelPicker } from './model-picker';
import { ChatComposer } from './chat-composer';
import { MessageList } from './message-list';
import { EmptyChatState } from './empty-chat-state';
import { useChatStream } from '@/features/chat/hooks/use-chat-stream';
import { useChatStore } from '@/features/chat/store';
import type { Tables } from '@/types/database';

type Props = {
  models: Tables<'ai_models'>[];
  initialModel: string;
  conversationId?: string;
  initialMessages?: Array<{
    id: string;
    role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system' | 'tool';
    content: string;
    created_at: string;
  }>;
  systemPrompt?: string | null;
};

export function ChatShell({
  models,
  initialModel,
  conversationId,
  initialMessages = [],
  systemPrompt,
}: Props) {
  const [model, setModel] = useState(initialModel);
  const { send, stop } = useChatStream();
  const { messages, setMessages, streaming, setActiveConversation, activeConversationId } =
    useChatStore();

  useEffect(() => {
    setActiveConversation(conversationId ?? null);
    setMessages(
      initialMessages.map((m) => ({
        id: m.id,
        role: m.role,
        content: m.content,
        status: 'complete' as const,
        createdAt: m.created_at,
      })),
    );
  }, [conversationId, initialMessages, setActiveConversation, setMessages]);

  const hasMessages = messages.length > 0;

  const handleSubmit = (text: string) => {
    void send({
      content: text,
      model,
      conversationId: conversationId ?? activeConversationId ?? undefined,
      systemPrompt: systemPrompt ?? undefined,
    });
  };

  const handleRegenerate = useMemo(
    () => (index: number) => {
      const prev = messages[index - 1];
      if (!prev || prev.role !== 'user') {
        toast.error('لا يمكن إعادة التوليد');
        return;
      }
      void send({
        content: prev.content,
        model,
        conversationId: conversationId ?? activeConversationId ?? undefined,
        systemPrompt: systemPrompt ?? undefined,
      });
    },
    [activeConversationId, conversationId, messages, model, send, systemPrompt],
  );

  return (
    <div className="flex h-[calc(100dvh-4rem)] flex-col">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface)] px-4 py-2 sm:px-6">
        <ModelPicker models={models} value={model} onChange={setModel} disabled={streaming} />
        <span className="text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          {messages.length > 0 && `${messages.length} رسالة`}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto scrollbar-thin">
        {hasMessages ? (
          <MessageList onRegenerate={handleRegenerate} />
        ) : (
          <EmptyChatState onPick={handleSubmit} />
        )}
      </div>

      <ChatComposer
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        onStop={stop}
        streaming={streaming}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/app/(app)/chat/new/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { AiModelsRepository } from '@/server/repositories/ai-models.repo';
import { ChatShell } from '@/components/chat/chat-shell';
import { FREE_TIER } from '@/config/constants';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'محادثة جديدة',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function NewChatPage() {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const models = await new AiModelsRepository(supabase).listEnabled();
  const defaultModel = models[0]?.slug ?? 'deepseek-chat';

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-0 sm:px-4">
      <ChatShell models={models} initialModel={defaultModel} />
      <p className="sr-only">الحد المجاني: {FREE_TIER.dailyMessages} رسالة يوميًا</p>
```

### `src/app/(app)/chat/[id]/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { AiModelsRepository } from '@/server/repositories/ai-models.repo';
import { ConversationService } from '@/server/services/conversation.service';
import { MessageService } from '@/server/services/message.service';
import { ChatShell } from '@/components/chat/chat-shell';
import { idParamSchema } from '@/lib/validation/common';

type PageProps = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: PageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { id } = await params;
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return { title: 'محادثة' };
  const { data } = await supabase.from('conversations').select('title').eq('id', id).maybeSingle();
  return {
    title: data?.title ?? 'محادثة',
    robots: { index: false, follow: false },
  };
}

export default async function ConversationPage({ params }: PageProps) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const parsed = idParamSchema.safeParse({ id });
  if (!parsed.success) notFound();

  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) notFound();

  let conversation;
  try {
    conversation = await new ConversationService(supabase).getOwnedOrThrow(id, user.id);
  } catch {
    notFound();
  }

  const [models, messagesResult] = await Promise.all([
    new AiModelsRepository(supabase).listEnabled(),
    new MessageService(supabase).list(user.id, id),
  ]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-0 sm:px-4">
      <ChatShell
        models={models}
        initialModel={conversation.model}
        conversationId={conversation.id}
        systemPrompt={conversation.system_prompt}
        initialMessages={messagesResult.items.map((m) => ({
          id: m.id,
          role: m.role as 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system' | 'tool',
          content: m.content,
          created_at: m.created_at,
        }))}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 📁 لوحة التحكم

### `src/app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { MessageSquare, BarChart3, Bell, KeyRound, Plus, Archive } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { requireUser } from '@/server/guards/require-user';
import { ConversationService } from '@/server/services/conversation.service';
import { UsageService } from '@/server/services/usage.service';
import { NotificationService } from '@/server/services/notification.service';
import { formatNumber } from '@/lib/utils/format';
import { formatRelative } from '@/lib/utils/date';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'لوحة التحكم',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function DashboardPage() {
  const { user, supabase } = await requireUser();
  const [convs, quota, unread] = await Promise.all([
    new ConversationService(supabase).list({ userId: user.id, page: 1, pageSize: 6 }),
    new UsageService(supabase).checkQuota(user.id),
    new NotificationService(supabase).unreadCount(user.id),
  ]);

  const stats = [
    {
      icon: MessageSquare,
      label: 'المحادثات',
      value: formatNumber(convs.total),
      sub: 'إجمالي محادثاتك',
    },
    {
      icon: BarChart3,
      label: 'الرسائل اليوم',
      value: `${formatNumber(quota.used.messages)} / ${formatNumber(quota.limits.messages)}`,
      sub: 'الاستهلاك اليومي',
    },
    {
      icon: Bell,
      label: 'إشعارات غير مقروءة',
      value: formatNumber(unread),
      sub: 'انظر الإشعارات',
    },
    {
      icon: KeyRound,
      label: 'التوكنز اليوم',
      value: `${formatNumber(quota.used.tokens)} / ${formatNumber(quota.limits.tokens)}`,
      sub: 'من الحد اليومي',
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-8 px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">لوحة التحكم</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
            مرحبًا، استعرض نشاطك وابدأ محادثة جديدة.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Link href="/chat/new">
          <Button className="gap-2">
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
            محادثة جديدة
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </header>

      <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {stats.map((s) => {
          const Icon = s.icon;
          return (
            <Card key={s.label}>
              <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
                <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                  {s.label}
                </CardTitle>
                <Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-[color:var(--muted)]" />
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <div className="text-2xl font-semibold">{s.value}</div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">{s.sub}</p>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </section>

      <section>
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">أحدث المحادثات</h2>
          <Link
            href="/library"
            className="text-sm text-[color:var(--accent)] hover:underline"
          >
            عرض الكل
          </Link>
        </div>
        {convs.items.length === 0 ? (
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 py-12 text-center">
              <Archive className="h-10 w-10 text-[color:var(--muted)]" />
              <p className="text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">لا توجد محادثات بعد.</p>
              <Link href="/chat/new">
                <Button size="sm" className="gap-2">
                  <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  ابدأ أول محادثة
                </Button>
              </Link>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {convs.items.map((c) => (
              <Link key={c.id} href={`/chat/${c.id}`} className="group">
                <Card className="h-full transition-colors group-hover:border-[color:var(--accent)]/40">
                  <CardContent className="p-4">
                    <div className="mb-2 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <h3 className="line-clamp-1 font-medium">{c.title}</h3>
                      {c.is_favorite && <Badge variant="secondary">مثبتة</Badge>}
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                      {c.message_count} رسالة ·{' '}
                      {c.last_message_at ? formatRelative(c.last_message_at) : 'جديدة'}
                    </p>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 📁 البحث، المكتبة، الإشعارات

### `src/app/(app)/search/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { SearchClient } from '@/components/search/search-client';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'البحث',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default function SearchPage() {
  return <SearchClient />;
}
```

### `src/components/search/search-client.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Search, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { useDebounce } from '@/hooks/use-debounce';
import { truncate } from '@/lib/utils/format';
import { formatDateTime } from '@/lib/utils/date';

type Result = {
  id: string;
  content: string;
  role: string;
  created_at: string;
  conversation_id: string;
  model: string | null;
  conversations: { id: string; title: string } | null;
};

export function SearchClient() {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const debounced = useDebounce(q, 300);
  const [results, setResults] = useState<Result[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [searched, setSearched] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (debounced.trim().length < 2) {
      setResults([]);
      setSearched(false);
      return;
    }
    let cancelled = false;
    setLoading(true);
    (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(debounced)}&pageSize=30`);
        const json = await res.json();
        if (!cancelled && json.success) setResults(json.data.items);
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
          setSearched(true);
        }
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [debounced]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">البحث في المحادثات</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          ابحث في نصوص كل رسائلك.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-[color:var(--muted)] ltr:left-3 rtl:right-3" />
        <Input
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="ابحث في رسائلك…"
          autoFocus
          className="ltr:pl-9 rtl:pr-9"
        />
      </div>

      {loading && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
          جارٍ البحث…
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && searched && results.length === 0 && (
        <p className="text-center text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          لا توجد نتائج مطابقة.
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {results.map((r) => (
          <Link key={r.id} href={`/chat/${r.conversation_id}`}>
            <Card className="transition-colors hover:border-[color:var(--accent)]/40">
              <CardContent className="p-4">
                <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                  <Badge variant="secondary">{r.role === 'user' ? 'أنت' : 'AI'}</Badge>
                  <span>{r.conversations?.title ?? 'محادثة'}</span>
                  <span>·</span>
                  <span>{formatDateTime(r.created_at)}</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed">{truncate(r.content, 240)}</p>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/hooks/use-debounce.ts`
```ts
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export function useDebounce<T>(value: T, delay = 300): T {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(value), delay);
    return () => clearTimeout(id);
  }, [value, delay]);
  return debounced;
}
```

### `src/app/(app)/library/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Star, Archive as ArchiveIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs';
import { requireUser } from '@/server/guards/require-user';
import { ConversationService } from '@/server/services/conversation.service';
import { formatRelative } from '@/lib/utils/date';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'المكتبة',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function LibraryPage() {
  const { user, supabase } = await requireUser();
  const service = new ConversationService(supabase);
  const [all, favorites, archived] = await Promise.all([
    service.list({ userId: user.id, page: 1, pageSize: 50 }),
    service.list({ userId: user.id, page: 1, pageSize: 50, onlyFavorites: true }),
    service.list({ userId: user.id, page: 1, pageSize: 50, onlyArchived: true }),
  ]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <header className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">المكتبة</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          كل محادثاتك، مثبتاتك، والمؤرشفة.
        </p>
      </header>

      <Tabs defaultValue="all">
        <TabsList>
          <TabsTrigger value="all">الكل ({all.total})</TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="favorites">المثبتة ({favorites.total})</TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="archived">المؤرشفة ({archived.total})</TabsTrigger>
        </TabsList>

        {[
          { key: 'all', items: all.items, empty: 'لا توجد محادثات بعد.' },
          { key: 'favorites', items: favorites.items, empty: 'لا توجد محادثات مثبتة.' },
          { key: 'archived', items: archived.items, empty: 'لا توجد محادثات مؤرشفة.' },
        ].map(({ key, items, empty }) => (
          <TabsContent key={key} value={key} className="mt-6">
            {items.length === 0 ? (
              <Card>
                <CardContent className="py-12 text-center text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                  {empty}
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                {items.map((c) => (
                  <Link key={c.id} href={`/chat/${c.id}`} className="group">
                    <Card className="h-full transition-colors group-hover:border-[color:var(--accent)]/40">
                      <CardContent className="p-4">
                        <div className="mb-2 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                          <h3 className="line-clamp-1 font-medium">{c.title}</h3>
                          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
                            {c.is_favorite && (
                              <Star className="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-[color:var(--accent)] text-[color:var(--accent)]" />
                            )}
                            {c.is_archived && <ArchiveIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[color:var(--muted)]" />}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <p className="mb-3 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                          {c.message_count} رسالة ·{' '}
                          {c.last_message_at ? formatRelative(c.last_message_at) : 'جديدة'}
                        </p>
                        <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-[10px]">{c.model}</Badge>
                      </CardContent>
                    </Card>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </TabsContent>
        ))}
      </Tabs>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/ui/tabs.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import * as TabsPrimitive from '@radix-ui/react-tabs';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';

const Tabs = TabsPrimitive.Root;

const TabsList = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof TabsPrimitive.List>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof TabsPrimitive.List>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <TabsPrimitive.List
    ref={ref}
    className={cn(
      'inline-flex h-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface)] p-1 text-[color:var(--muted)]',
      className,
    )}
    {...props}
  />
));
TabsList.displayName = TabsPrimitive.List.displayName;

const TabsTrigger = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof TabsPrimitive.Trigger>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof TabsPrimitive.Trigger>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <TabsPrimitive.Trigger
    ref={ref}
    className={cn(
      'inline-flex items-center justify-center whitespace-nowrap rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-all',
      'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[color:var(--ring)]',
      'disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50',
      'data-[state=active]:bg-[color:var(--accent)] data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:shadow-sm',
      className,
    )}
    {...props}
  />
));
TabsTrigger.displayName = TabsPrimitive.Trigger.displayName;

const TabsContent = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof TabsPrimitive.Content>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof TabsPrimitive.Content>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <TabsPrimitive.Content
    ref={ref}
    className={cn('focus-visible:outline-none', className)}
    {...props}
  />
));
TabsContent.displayName = TabsPrimitive.Content.displayName;

export { Tabs, TabsList, TabsTrigger, TabsContent };
```

### `src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import * as DropdownMenuPrimitive from '@radix-ui/react-dropdown-menu';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';

const DropdownMenu = DropdownMenuPrimitive.Root;
const DropdownMenuTrigger = DropdownMenuPrimitive.Trigger;
const DropdownMenuGroup = DropdownMenuPrimitive.Group;

const DropdownMenuContent = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Content>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Content>
>(({ className, sideOffset = 4, ...props }, ref) => (
  <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Portal>
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Content
      ref={ref}
      sideOffset={sideOffset}
      className={cn(
        'z-50 min-w-[10rem] overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface)] p-1 text-[color:var(--foreground)] shadow-lg',
        'data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  </DropdownMenuPrimitive.Portal>
));
DropdownMenuContent.displayName = DropdownMenuPrimitive.Content.displayName;

const DropdownMenuItem = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Item>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Item>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Item
    ref={ref}
    className={cn(
      'relative flex cursor-pointer select-none items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none transition-colors',
      'focus:bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)] data-[disabled]:pointer-events-none data-[disabled]:opacity-50',
      className,
    )}
    {...props}
  />
));
DropdownMenuItem.displayName = DropdownMenuPrimitive.Item.displayName;

const DropdownMenuLabel = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Label>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Label>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Label
    ref={ref}
    className={cn('px-2 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-[color:var(--muted)]', className)}
    {...props}
  />
));
DropdownMenuLabel.displayName = DropdownMenuPrimitive.Label.displayName;

const DropdownMenuSeparator = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Separator>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Separator>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Separator
    ref={ref}
    className={cn('-mx-1 my-1 h-px bg-[color:var(--border)]', className)}
    {...props}
  />
));
DropdownMenuSeparator.displayName = DropdownMenuPrimitive.Separator.displayName;

export {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuGroup,
};
```

### `src/app/(app)/notifications/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { requireUser } from '@/server/guards/require-user';
import { NotificationService } from '@/server/services/notification.service';
import { NotificationsClient } from '@/components/notifications/notifications-client';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'الإشعارات',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function NotificationsPage() {
  const { user, supabase } = await requireUser();
  const service = new NotificationService(supabase);
  const [result, unread] = await Promise.all([
    service.list(user.id, { page: 1, pageSize: 50 }),
    service.unreadCount(user.id),
  ]);

  return <NotificationsClient items={result.items} unread={unread} />;
}
```

### `src/components/notifications/notifications-client.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Bell, CheckCheck, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';
import { formatRelative } from '@/lib/utils/date';
import type { Tables } from '@/types/database';

type Props = { items: Tables<'notifications'>[]; unread: number };

export function NotificationsClient({ items: initial, unread: initialUnread }: Props) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState(initial);
  const [unread, setUnread] = useState(initialUnread);

  const markAll = async () => {
    await fetch('/api/notifications', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ all: true }),
    });
    setItems((prev) => prev.map((n) => ({ ...n, is_read: true, read_at: new Date().toISOString() })));
    setUnread(0);
    toast.success('تم تعليم الكل كمقروء');
  };

  const markOne = async (id: string) => {
    await fetch('/api/notifications', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ ids: [id] }),
    });
    setItems((prev) => prev.map((n) => (n.id === id ? { ...n, is_read: true } : n)));
    setUnread((u) => Math.max(u - 1, 0));
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <header className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">الإشعارات</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
            {unread > 0 ? `لديك ${unread} إشعار غير مقروء` : 'كل الإشعارات مقروءة'}
          </p>
        </div>
        {unread > 0 && (
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={markAll} className="gap-2">
            <CheckCheck className="h-4 w-4" />
            تعليم الكل كمقروء
          </Button>
        )}
      </header>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 py-12 text-center">
            <Bell className="h-10 w-10 text-[color:var(--muted)]" />
            <p className="text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">لا توجد إشعارات بعد.</p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {items.map((n) => (
            <div
              key={n.id}
              className={cn(
                'group flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-[color:var(--border)] p-4 transition-colors',
                n.is_read ? 'bg-[color:var(--surface)]' : 'bg-[color:var(--accent)]/5',
              )}
            >
              <div
                className={cn(
                  'mt-1 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full',
                  n.is_read ? 'bg-[color:var(--muted)]/40' : 'bg-[color:var(--accent)]',
                )}
              />
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="font-medium">{n.title}</h3>
                  {!n.is_read && <Badge variant="secondary">جديد</Badge>}
                </div>
                {n.body && <p className="text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">{n.body}</p>}
                <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                  <span>{formatRelative(n.created_at)}</span>
                  {n.action_url && (
                    <Link href={n.action_url} className="text-[color:var(--accent)] hover:underline">
                      فتح
                    </Link>
                  )}
                  {!n.is_read && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => markOne(n.id)}
                      className="hover:text-[color:var(--foreground)]"
                    >
                      تعليم كمقروء
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" aria-label="حذف" className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">
                <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </Button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/settings/settings-nav.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { User, Palette, Shield, Globe } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';

const items = [
  { label: 'الملف الشخصي', href: '/settings/profile', icon: User },
  { label: 'المظهر', href: '/settings/appearance', icon: Palette },
  { label: 'الأمان', href: '/settings/security', icon: Shield },
  { label: 'اللغة والمنطقة', href: '/settings/locale', icon: Globe },
];

export function SettingsNav() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  return (
    <nav aria-label="إعدادات" className="flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto lg:flex-col">
      {items.map((item) => {
        const active = pathname === item.href;
        const Icon = item.icon;
        return (
          <Link
            key={item.href}
            href={item.href}
            className={cn(
              'flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors',
              active
                ? 'bg-[color:var(--accent)]/12 text-[color:var(--accent)]'
                : 'text-[color:var(--muted)] hover:bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)] hover:text-[color:var(--foreground)]',
            )}
          >
            <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
            {item.label}
          </Link>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
export const metadata: Metadata = {

  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function ProfileSettingsPage() {
  const { user, supabase } = await requireUser();
  const profile = await new ProfilesRepository(supabase).findById(user.id);

  return (
    <ProfileForm
      initialValues={{
        fullName: profile?.full_name ?? '',
```

### `src/components/settings/profile-form.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { profileSchema, type ProfileInput } from '@/lib/validation/settings';

type Props = {
  initialValues: ProfileInput;
  email: string;
};

export function ProfileForm({ initialValues, email }: Props) {
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState: { errors, isDirty } } = useForm<ProfileInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(profileSchema),
    defaultValues: initialValues,
  });

  async function onSubmit(data: ProfileInput) {
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .update({
          full_name: data.fullName,
          username: data.username,
          bio: data.bio || null,
          avatar_url: data.avatarUrl || null,
        })
        .eq('id', (await supabase.auth.getUser()).data.user!.id);
      if (error) {
        toast.error(error.message.includes('username') ? 'اسم المستخدم مستخدم بالفعل' : 'تعذّر الحفظ');
        return;
      }
      toast.success('تم حفظ التغييرات');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>الملف الشخصي</CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>حدّث معلومات حسابك العامة.</CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-5" noValidate>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label>البريد الإلكتروني</Label>
            <Input value={email} disabled />
            <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">لا يمكن تغيير البريد الإلكتروني.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="fullName">الاسم الكامل</Label>
            <Input id="fullName" {...register('fullName')} aria-invalid={!!errors.fullName} />
            {errors.fullName && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.fullName.message}</p>}
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="username">اسم المستخدم</Label>
            <Input id="username" {...register('username')} aria-invalid={!!errors.username} />
            {errors.username && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.username.message}</p>}
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="avatarUrl">رابط الصورة الرمزية</Label>
            <Input id="avatarUrl" type="url" placeholder="https://…" {...register('avatarUrl')} />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="bio">نبذة</Label>
            <Textarea id="bio" rows={3} maxLength={280} {...register('bio')} />
            {errors.bio && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.bio.message}</p>}
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" disabled={submitting || !isDirty}>
            {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : null}
            حفظ التغييرات
          </Button>
        </form>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';
import { Sun, Moon, Monitor } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';
import { useMounted } from '@/hooks/use-mounted';

const options = [
  { value: 'light', label: 'فاتح', icon: Sun },
  { value: 'dark', label: 'داكن', icon: Moon },
  { value: 'system', label: 'النظام', icon: Monitor },
] as const;

export function AppearanceForm() {
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();
  const mounted = useMounted();

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>المظهر</CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>اختر الوضع الذي يريح عينيك.</CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {options.map((opt) => {
            const Icon = opt.icon;
            const active = mounted && theme === opt.value;
            return (
              <button
                key={opt.value}
                onClick={() => setTheme(opt.value)}
                className={cn(
                  'flex flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border p-4 transition-colors',
                  active
                    ? 'border-[color:var(--accent)] bg-[color:var(--accent)]/5'
                    : 'border-[color:var(--border)] hover:bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)]',
                )}
              >
                <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                <span className="text-sm font-medium">{opt.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}
```
