### `src/components/settings/security-form.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { changePasswordSchema, type ChangePasswordInput } from '@/lib/validation/settings';

export function SecurityForm() {
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState: { errors }, reset } = useForm<ChangePasswordInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(changePasswordSchema),
    defaultValues: { currentPassword: '', newPassword: '', confirmPassword: '' },
  });

  async function onSubmit(data: ChangePasswordInput) {
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user?.email) { toast.error('تعذّر التحقق'); return; }

      const { error: signInErr } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email: user.email,
        password: data.currentPassword,
      });
      if (signInErr) { toast.error('كلمة المرور الحالية غير صحيحة'); return; }

      const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: data.newPassword });
      if (error) { toast.error('تعذّر تحديث كلمة المرور'); return; }
      toast.success('تم تحديث كلمة المرور');
      reset();
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>تغيير كلمة المرور</CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>استخدم كلمة مرور قوية لا تستخدمها في مكان آخر.</CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-5" noValidate>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="currentPassword">كلمة المرور الحالية</Label>
            <Input id="currentPassword" type="password" autoComplete="current-password" {...register('currentPassword')} />
            {errors.currentPassword && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.currentPassword.message}</p>}
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="newPassword">كلمة المرور الجديدة</Label>
            <Input id="newPassword" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" {...register('newPassword')} />
            {errors.newPassword && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.newPassword.message}</p>}
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="confirmPassword">تأكيد كلمة المرور</Label>
            <Input id="confirmPassword" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" {...register('confirmPassword')} />
            {errors.confirmPassword && <p className="text-xs text-[color:var(--danger)]">{errors.confirmPassword.message}</p>}
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" disabled={submitting}>
            {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : null}
            تحديث كلمة المرور
          </Button>
        </form>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/settings/locale-form.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { localeSchema, type LocaleInput } from '@/lib/validation/settings';

const locales = [
  { value: 'ar', label: 'العربية' },
  { value: 'en', label: 'English' },
] as const;

const timezones = [
  'Asia/Baghdad',
  'Asia/Riyadh',
  'Asia/Dubai',
  'Africa/Cairo',
  'Africa/Casablanca',
  'Europe/London',
  'Europe/Paris',
  'America/New_York',
  'UTC',
];

type Props = { initialLocale: 'ar' | 'en'; initialTimezone: string };

export function LocaleForm({ initialLocale, initialTimezone }: Props) {
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState: { errors, isDirty } } = useForm<LocaleInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(localeSchema),
    defaultValues: { locale: initialLocale, timezone: initialTimezone },
  });

  async function onSubmit(data: LocaleInput) {
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) return;
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .update({ locale: data.locale, timezone: data.timezone })
        .eq('id', user.id);
      if (error) { toast.error('تعذّر الحفظ'); return; }
      toast.success('تم حفظ التغييرات');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>اللغة والمنطقة الزمنية</CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>اختر لغتك والمنطقة الزمنية التي تريد.</CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-5" noValidate>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="locale">اللغة</Label>
            <select
              id="locale"
              {...register('locale')}
              className="flex h-10 w-full rounded-md border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface)] px-3 py-2 text-sm"
            >
              {locales.map((l) => (
                <option key={l.value} value={l.value}>{l.label}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="timezone">المنطقة الزمنية</Label>
            <select
              id="timezone"
              {...register('timezone')}
              className="flex h-10 w-full rounded-md border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface)] px-3 py-2 text-sm"
            >
              {timezones.map((tz) => (
                <option key={tz} value={tz}>{tz}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" disabled={submitting || !isDirty}>
            {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : null}
            حفظ التغييرات
          </Button>
        </form>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

## 📁 صفحة الاستهلاك ومفاتيح API

### `src/app/(app)/usage/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { requireUser } from '@/server/guards/require-user';
import { UsageService } from '@/server/services/usage.service';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { formatNumber } from '@/lib/utils/format';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'الاستهلاك',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function UsagePage() {
  const { user, supabase } = await requireUser();
  const service = new UsageService(supabase);
  const [quota, daily] = await Promise.all([service.checkQuota(user.id), service.userDaily(user.id, 30)]);

  const messagePct = quota.limits.messages ? (quota.used.messages / quota.limits.messages) * 100 : 0;
  const tokenPct = quota.limits.tokens ? (quota.used.tokens / quota.limits.tokens) * 100 : 0;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl space-y-6 px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <header>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">الاستهلاك</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          راقب استخدامك اليومي من الحد المجاني.
        </p>
      </header>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-[color:var(--muted)]">الرسائل اليوم</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="mb-2 text-2xl font-semibold">
              {formatNumber(quota.used.messages)} / {formatNumber(quota.limits.messages)}
            </div>
            <div className="h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)]">
              <div
                className="h-full bg-[color:var(--accent)] transition-all"
                style={{ width: `${Math.min(messagePct, 100)}%` }}
              />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
              متبقٍ: {formatNumber(quota.remaining.messages)} رسالة
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-[color:var(--muted)]">التوكنز اليوم</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="mb-2 text-2xl font-semibold">
              {formatNumber(quota.used.tokens)} / {formatNumber(quota.limits.tokens)}
            </div>
            <div className="h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)]">
              <div
                className="h-full bg-[color:var(--accent-blue)] transition-all"
                style={{ width: `${Math.min(tokenPct, 100)}%` }}
              />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
              متبقٍ: {formatNumber(quota.remaining.tokens)} توكن
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>آخر 30 يومًا</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          {daily.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-center text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">لا يوجد نشاط بعد.</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {daily
                .slice()
                .reverse()
                .map((d) => {
                  const max = Math.max(...daily.map((x) => x.tokens), 1);
                  const pct = (d.tokens / max) * 100;
                  return (
                    <div key={d.date} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs">
                      <span className="w-24 shrink-0 font-mono text-[color:var(--muted)]">{d.date}</span>
                      <div className="h-2 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)]">
                        <div
                          className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-[color:var(--accent)] to-[color:var(--accent-blue)]"
                          style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                        />
                      </div>
                      <span className="w-20 shrink-0 text-end">{formatNumber(d.tokens)}</span>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/api-keys/api-keys-client.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { KeyRound, Copy, Check, Loader2, Plus, Ban } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { formatDateTime } from '@/lib/utils/date';

type ApiKeyItem = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  key_prefix: string;
  scopes: string[];
  last_used_at: string | null;
  expires_at: string | null;
  revoked_at: string | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type Props = { initialKeys: ApiKeyItem[] };

export function ApiKeysClient({ initialKeys }: Props) {
  const [keys, setKeys] = useState(initialKeys);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const create = async () => {
    if (name.trim().length < 2) { toast.error('اسم المفتاح قصير'); return; }
    setCreating(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/api-keys', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name: name.trim(), scopes: ['chat:read', 'chat:write'] }),
      });
      const json = await res.json();
      if (!json.success) { toast.error(json.error?.message ?? 'تعذّر الإنشاء'); return; }
      setRevealed(json.data.fullKey);
      setName('');
      // Refresh list
      const list = await fetch('/api/api-keys').then((r) => r.json());
      if (list.success) setKeys(list.data);
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  };

  const copyKey = async (key: string) => {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(key);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
  };

  const revoke = async (id: string) => {
    if (!confirm('هل أنت متأكد من إلغاء هذا المفتاح؟ لا يمكن التراجع.')) return;
    const res = await fetch(`/api/api-keys/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    if (res.ok) {
      toast.success('تم إلغاء المفتاح');
      setKeys((prev) => prev.map((k) => (k.id === id ? { ...k, revoked_at: new Date().toISOString() } : k)));
    } else toast.error('تعذّر الإلغاء');
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl space-y-6 px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <header>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">مفاتيح API</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          أنشئ مفاتيح لاستخدام Nados AI في تطبيقاتك. لن يظهر المفتاح الكامل إلا مرة واحدة.
        </p>
      </header>

      {revealed && (
        <Card className="border-[color:var(--accent)]/40">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="text-base">انسخ المفتاح الآن</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>
              لن تستطيع رؤيته مرة أخرى. احفظه في مكان آمن.
            </CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-[color:var(--border)] bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)] p-3">
              <code dir="ltr" className="flex-1 overflow-x-auto font-mono text-xs">{revealed}</code>
              <Button size="icon-sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => copyKey(revealed)} aria-label="نسخ">
                {copied ? <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[color:var(--success)]" /> : <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
              </Button>
            </div>
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => setRevealed(null)}>تم، أخفِ المفتاح</Button>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="text-base">إنشاء مفتاح جديد</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
            <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="keyName">اسم المفتاح</Label>
              <Input
                id="keyName"
                placeholder="مثال: تكامل موقعي"
                value={name}
                onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                maxLength={60}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-end">
              <Button onClick={create} disabled={creating} className="gap-2">
                {creating ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />}
                إنشاء
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="text-base">المفاتيح الحالية</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          {keys.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">لا توجد مفاتيح بعد.</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {keys.map((k) => {
                const revoked = !!k.revoked_at;
                const expired = k.expires_at ? new Date(k.expires_at) < new Date() : false;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={k.id}
                    className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[color:var(--border)] p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between"
                  >
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <KeyRound className="h-4 w-4 text-[color:var(--accent)]" />
                        <span className="font-medium">{k.name}</span>
                        {revoked && <Badge variant="danger">ملغى</Badge>}
                        {expired && !revoked && <Badge variant="danger">منتهي</Badge>}
                        {!revoked && !expired && <Badge variant="success">نشط</Badge>}
                      </div>
                      <p className="font-mono text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                        {k.key_prefix}_…
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                        آخر استخدام: {k.last_used_at ? formatDateTime(k.last_used_at) : 'أبدًا'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    {!revoked && (
                      <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => revoke(k.id)} className="gap-2">
                        <Ban className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        إلغاء
                      </Button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 📁 لوحة الإدارة

### `src/app/(admin)/admin/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { Users, MessageSquare, Activity, Cpu } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { requireAdmin } from '@/server/guards/require-admin';
import { UsageService } from '@/server/services/usage.service';
import { formatNumber } from '@/lib/utils/format';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'لوحة الإدارة',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const { supabase } = await requireAdmin();

  const [users, conversations, messages, models, platform] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from('profiles').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }),
    supabase.from('conversations').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }),
    supabase.from('messages').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }),
    supabase.from('ai_models').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('is_enabled', true),
    new UsageService(supabase).platformDaily(14),
  ]);

  const stats = [
    { icon: Users, label: 'المستخدمون', value: formatNumber(users.count ?? 0) },
    { icon: MessageSquare, label: 'المحادثات', value: formatNumber(conversations.count ?? 0) },
    { icon: Activity, label: 'الرسائل', value: formatNumber(messages.count ?? 0) },
    { icon: Cpu, label: 'النماذج المفعّلة', value: formatNumber(models.count ?? 0) },
  ];

  const totals = platform.daily.reduce(
    (acc, d) => ({ tokens: acc.tokens + d.tokens, requests: acc.requests + d.requests }),
    { tokens: 0, requests: 0 },
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8">
      <header>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">نظرة عامة</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          مؤشرات المنصة الرئيسية.
        </p>
      </header>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {stats.map((s) => {
          const Icon = s.icon;
          return (
            <Card key={s.label}>
              <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
                <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                  {s.label}
                </CardTitle>
                <Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-[color:var(--muted)]" />
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <div className="text-2xl font-semibold">{s.value}</div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="text-base">آخر 14 يومًا</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            {platform.daily.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="py-4 text-center text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">لا توجد بيانات بعد.</p>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div className="mb-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
                  <div className="rounded-md bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)] p-3">
                    <div className="text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">إجمالي التوكنز</div>
                    <div className="mt-1 font-semibold">{formatNumber(totals.tokens)}</div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="rounded-md bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)] p-3">
                    <div className="text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">إجمالي الطلبات</div>
                    <div className="mt-1 font-semibold">{formatNumber(totals.requests)}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  {platform.daily.slice(-7).map((d) => {
                    const max = Math.max(...platform.daily.map((x) => x.tokens), 1);
                    const pct = (d.tokens / max) * 100;
                    return (
                      <div key={d.date} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                        <span className="w-24 shrink-0 font-mono text-[color:var(--muted)]">{d.date}</span>
                        <div className="h-2 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)]">
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-[color:var(--accent)]"
                            style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="text-base">حسب النموذج</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            {platform.byModel.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="py-4 text-center text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">لا توجد بيانات.</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {platform.byModel.map((m) => (
                  <div key={m.model} className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                    <span className="font-mono text-xs">{m.model}</span>
                    <span className="text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                      {formatNumber(m.requests)} طلب · {formatNumber(m.tokens)} توكن
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/app/(admin)/admin/users/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { requireAdmin } from '@/server/guards/require-admin';
import { ProfilesRepository } from '@/server/repositories/profiles.repo';
import { UsersTable } from '@/components/admin/users-table';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'إدارة المستخدمين',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

type Props = { searchParams: Promise<{ page?: string; q?: string }> };

export default async function AdminUsersPage({ searchParams }: Props) {
  const { supabase } = await requireAdmin();
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const page = Number(sp.page ?? 1);
  const search = sp.q;

  const result = await new ProfilesRepository(supabase).list({ page, pageSize: 25, search });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8">
      <header>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">المستخدمون</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
          {result.total} مستخدم إجمالي.
        </p>
      </header>
      <UsersTable users={result.items} page={page} total={result.total} search={search} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/admin/users-table.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from '@/components/ui/avatar';
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { formatDateTime } from '@/lib/utils/date';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import type { Tables } from '@/types/database';

type Props = {
  users: Tables<'profiles'>[];
  page: number;
  total: number;
  search?: string;
};

export function UsersTable({ users: initial, page, total, search }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [users, setUsers] = useState(initial);
  const [q, setQ] = useState(search ?? '');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const submitSearch = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    router.push(`/admin/users?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&page=1`);
  };

  const toggleActive = async (u: Tables<'profiles'>) => {
    setBusy(u.id);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      await supabase.from('profiles').update({ is_active: !u.is_active }).eq('id', u.id);
      setUsers((prev) => prev.map((x) => (x.id === u.id ? { ...x, is_active: !x.is_active } : x)));
      toast.success('تم التحديث');
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const setRole = async (u: Tables<'profiles'>, role: 'user' | 'admin') => {
    setBusy(u.id);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      await supabase.from('profiles').update({ role }).eq('id', u.id);
      setUsers((prev) => prev.map((x) => (x.id === u.id ? { ...x, role } : x)));
      toast.success('تم تحديث الدور');
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / 25));

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardContent className="space-y-4 p-4">
        <form onSubmit={submitSearch} className="flex gap-2">
          <Input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="ابحث بالبريد أو اسم المستخدم…"
          />
          <Button type="submit" variant="outline">بحث</Button>
        </form>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-[color:var(--border)] text-start text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                <th className="py-3 text-start">المستخدم</th>
                <th className="py-3 text-start">الدور</th>
                <th className="py-3 text-start">الحالة</th>
                <th className="py-3 text-start">التسجيل</th>
                <th className="py-3" />
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {users.map((u) => (
                <tr key={u.id} className="border-b border-[color:var(--border)] last:border-0">
                  <td className="py-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <Avatar className="h-8 w-8">
                        {u.avatar_url ? <AvatarImage src={u.avatar_url} alt="" /> : null}
                        <AvatarFallback>
                          {(u.full_name ?? u.email ?? 'U').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()}
                        </AvatarFallback>
                      </Avatar>
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-medium">{u.full_name ?? '—'}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">{u.email}</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3">
                    <Badge variant={u.role === 'user' ? 'secondary' : 'default'}>{u.role}</Badge>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3">
                    <Badge variant={u.is_active ? 'success' : 'danger'}>
                      {u.is_active ? 'نشط' : 'معطّل'}
                    </Badge>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                    {formatDateTime(u.created_at)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3">
                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-1">
                      <Button
                        size="sm"
                        variant="outline"
                        onClick={() => toggleActive(u)}
                        disabled={busy === u.id}
                      >
                        {busy === u.id ? <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" /> : u.is_active ? 'تعطيل' : 'تفعيل'}
                      </Button>
                      {u.role !== 'owner' && (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="ghost"
                          onClick={() => setRole(u, u.role === 'admin' ? 'user' : 'admin')}
                          disabled={busy === u.id}
                        >
                          {u.role === 'admin' ? 'إزالة الإدارة' : 'ترقية لإداري'}
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        {totalPages > 1 && (
          <div className="flex justify-center gap-1">
            {Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => i + 1).map((p) => (
              <Button
                key={p}
                variant={p === page ? 'default' : 'outline'}
                size="icon-sm"
                onClick={() => router.push(`/admin/users?page=${p}${search ? `&q=${search}` : ''}`)}
              >
                {p}
              </Button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { requireAdmin } from '@/server/guards/require-admin';
```

### `src/components/admin/models-table.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import type { Tables } from '@/types/database';

export function ModelsTable({ models: initial }: { models: Tables<'ai_models'>[] }) {
  const [models, setModels] = useState(initial);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const toggle = async (m: Tables<'ai_models'>) => {
    setBusy(m.id);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      await supabase.from('ai_models').update({ is_enabled: !m.is_enabled }).eq('id', m.id);
      setModels((prev) => prev.map((x) => (x.id === m.id ? { ...x, is_enabled: !x.is_enabled } : x)));
      toast.success('تم التحديث');
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardContent className="p-0">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-[color:var(--border)] text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-start">النموذج</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-start">المزود</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-start">السياق</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-start">مفعّل</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {models.map((m) => (
                <tr key={m.id} className="border-b border-[color:var(--border)] last:border-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <div className="font-medium">{m.name}</div>
                    <div className="font-mono text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">{m.slug}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <Badge variant="secondary">{m.provider}</Badge>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                    {Intl.NumberFormat('ar').format(m.context_window)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <Switch
                      checked={m.is_enabled}
                      onCheckedChange={() => toggle(m)}
                      disabled={busy === m.id}
                      aria-label="تفعيل النموذج"
                    />
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/ui/switch.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import * as SwitchPrimitive from '@radix-ui/react-switch';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';

const Switch = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof SwitchPrimitive.Root>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof SwitchPrimitive.Root>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <SwitchPrimitive.Root
    ref={ref}
    className={cn(
      'peer inline-flex h-5 w-9 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center rounded-full border-2 border-transparent transition-colors',
      'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[color:var(--ring)] focus-visible:ring-offset-2',
      'disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50',
      'data-[state=checked]:bg-[color:var(--accent)] data-[state=unchecked]:bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)]',
      className,
    )}
    {...props}
  >
    <SwitchPrimitive.Thumb
      className={cn(
        'pointer-events-none block h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-white shadow-lg ring-0 transition-transform',
        'data-[state=checked]:translate-x-4 data-[state=unchecked]:translate-x-0',
      )}
    />
  </SwitchPrimitive.Root>
));
Switch.displayName = SwitchPrimitive.Root.displayName;

export { Switch };
```

### `src/app/(admin)/admin/contact/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { requireAdmin } from '@/server/guards/require-admin';
import { ContactRepository } from '@/server/repositories/contact.repo';
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { formatDateTime } from '@/lib/utils/date';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'رسائل التواصل',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function AdminContactPage() {
  const { supabase } = await requireAdmin();
  const { items } = await new ContactRepository(supabase).list({ page: 1, pageSize: 50 });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8">
      <header>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">رسائل التواصل</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">{items.length} رسالة.</p>
      </header>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="py-12 text-center text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">
            لا توجد رسائل بعد.
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {items.map((m) => (
            <Card key={m.id}>
              <CardContent className="p-4">
                <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                  <Badge variant={m.status === 'new' ? 'default' : 'secondary'}>{m.status}</Badge>
                  <span className="font-medium text-[color:var(--foreground)]">{m.name}</span>
                  <span>·</span>
                  <a href={`mailto:${m.email}`} className="text-[color:var(--accent)] hover:underline">{m.email}</a>
                  <span>·</span>
                  <span>{formatDateTime(m.created_at)}</span>
                </div>
                <h3 className="mb-1 font-medium">{m.subject}</h3>
                <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed text-[color:var(--muted)]">{m.message}</p>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 text-start">التاريخ</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 text-start">العملية</th>

                      {formatDateTime(log.created_at)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <Badge variant="secondary">{log.action}</Badge>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 font-mono text-xs text-[color:var(--muted)]">
                      {log.entity_type && log.entity_id
                        ? `${log.entity_type}:${log.entity_id.slice(0, 8)}…`
                        : '—'}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          {items.length === 0 && (
            <p className="py-12 text-center text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">لا توجد سجلات بعد.</p>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/app/(admin)/admin/usage/page.tsx`
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { requireAdmin } from '@/server/guards/require-admin';
import { UsageService } from '@/server/services/usage.service';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { formatNumber } from '@/lib/utils/format';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'مراقبة الاستهلاك',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function AdminUsagePage() {
  const { supabase } = await requireAdmin();
  const data = await new UsageService(supabase).platformDaily(30);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8">
      <header>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold sm:text-3xl">مراقبة الاستهلاك</h1>
      </header>
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="text-base">آخر 30 يومًا</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          {data.daily.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-[color:var(--muted)]">لا توجد بيانات.</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {data.daily.map((d) => {
                const max = Math.max(...data.daily.map((x) => x.tokens), 1);
                const pct = (d.tokens / max) * 100;
                return (
                  <div key={d.date} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs">
                    <span className="w-24 shrink-0 font-mono text-[color:var(--muted)]">{d.date}</span>
                    <div className="h-2 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[color:var(--surface-elevated)]">
                      <div className="h-full bg-[color:var(--accent)]" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
                    </div>
                    <span className="w-20 shrink-0 text-end">{formatNumber(d.tokens)}</span>
                    <span className="w-16 shrink-0 text-end text-[color:var(--muted)]">{d.users} مستخدم</span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/admin/notification-broadcast.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Loader2, Send } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';

export function NotificationBroadcast() {
  const [identifier, setIdentifier] = useState('');
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [body, setBody] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const send = async () => {
    if (!identifier.trim() || !title.trim()) {
      toast.error('أكمل الحقول المطلوبة');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const supabase = createClient();
      const { data: profile } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('id')
        .or(`username.eq.${identifier},email.eq.${identifier}`)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (!profile) { toast.error('المستخدم غير موجود'); return; }

      const { error } = await supabase.from('notifications').insert({
        user_id: profile.id,
        title: title.trim(),
        body: body.trim() || null,
        type: 'admin',
      });
      if (error) { toast.error('تعذّر الإرسال'); return; }
      toast.success('تم إرسال الإشعار');
      setTitle(''); setBody('');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="identifier">المستخدم (اسم المستخدم أو البريد)</Label>
        <Input id="identifier" value={identifier} onChange={(e) => setIdentifier(e.target.value)} placeholder="username أو email" />
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="title">العنوان</Label>
        <Input id="title" value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} maxLength={120} />
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="body">النص</Label>
        <Textarea id="body" value={body} onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)} rows={3} />
      </div>
      <Button onClick={send} disabled={submitting} className="gap-2">
        {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Send className="h-4 w-4" />}
        إرسال
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 📋 ملخص المرحلة 3 الكامل

### ✅ ما تم إنجازه

| الفئة | الملفات |
|-------|---------|
| **Migrations** | 13 ملف SQL (extensions, tables, indexes, triggers, RLS) |
| **Seed** | 4 نماذج AI |
| **AI Layer** | provider interface + 3 adapters (DeepSeek/OpenAI/Gemini) + factory |
| **Security** | hash, permissions, rate-limit, sanitize |
| **Validation** | 6 ملفات Zod (common, auth, chat, settings, api-keys, contact) |
| **Repositories** | 9 ملفات (profiles, conversations, messages, ai-models, usage, notifications, api-keys, contact, audit) |
| **Services** | 7 ملفات (audit, conversation, message, usage, notification, api-key, chat) |
| **Guards** | require-user, require-admin |
| **API Routes** | 10 نقاط: health, chat (SSE), conversations, [id], search, notifications, api-keys, [id], contact, v1/chat/completions, logout |
| **Auth Pages** | 6 صفحات: login, register, forgot-password, reset-password, verify-email, logout |
| **Auth Components** | 5 نماذج + layouts |
| **Marketing** | about, services, contact + form, faq + accordion, privacy, terms + robots + sitemap + manifest |
| **Chat** | store, stream hook, model-picker, composer, message-item, markdown, code-block, list, empty-state, shell + 2 صفحات |
| **Dashboard** | صفحة رئيسية + 4 بطاقات إحصاء |
| **Search** | client + debounce hook + API |
| **Library** | 3 تابات (all/favorites/archived) |
| **Notifications** | client + mark all/one read |
| **Settings** | layout + nav + 4 صفحات (profile/appearance/security/locale) |
| **Usage** | صفحة استهلاك مع مخطط 30 يومًا |
| **API Keys** | client كامل (create/reveal/revoke) |
| **Admin** | dashboard + users + models + contact + security-logs + usage + notifications + system |
| **UI Kit جديد** | tabs, dropdown-menu, switch |

**المجموع التراكمي: ~120 ملفًا.**

### 🚀 أوامر التشغيل الكامل

```bash
# 1. تثبيت الحزم
pnpm install

# 2. تشغيل Supabase محليًا (يتطلب Docker)
supabase start

# 3. تطبيق Migrations + Seed
supabase db reset

# 4. توليد الأنواع
pnpm supabase:gen-types

# 5. نسخ البيئة
cp .env.example .env.local
# املأ: NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL, NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, DEEPSEEK_API_KEY

# 6. تشغيل التطوير
pnpm dev
```

### 🧪 سيناريو اختبار يدوي كامل

1. **زيارة `/`** → صفحة رئيسية كاملة.
2. **`/about`, `/services`, `/faq`, `/contact`, `/privacy`, `/terms`** → كلها تعمل.
3. **`/register`** → إنشاء حساب → التحقق من البريد.
4. **`/login`** → دخول ناجح → تحويل إلى `/dashboard`.
5. **`/chat/new`** → إرسال رسالة → بث فوري → حفظ تلقائي → URL يصبح `/chat/[id]`.
6. **`/search`** → بحث في الرسائل → نتائج فورية.
7. **`/library`** → 3 تابات تعمل.
8. **`/notifications`** → إشعار الترحيب موجود، تعليم كمقروء.
9. **`/settings/profile`** → تعديل البيانات → حفظ.
10. **`/settings/appearance`** → تبديل الثيم.
11. **`/settings/security`** → تغيير كلمة المرور.
12. **`/api-keys`** → إنشاء مفتاح → يظهر مرة واحدة → إلغاء.
13. **`/usage`** → مخطط استهلاك.
14. **`/admin`** → فقط للمستخدم بدور admin. جرب تغيير دورك في DB.
15. **`/admin/users`** → تعطيل/تفعيل، ترقية.
16. **`/admin/models`** → تفعيل/تعطيل نموذج.
17. **`/admin/security-logs`** → يظهر سجل إنشاء API key.

### 📊 نسبة الإنجاز

- المرحلة 1: 100% ✅
- المرحلة 2: 100% ✅
- **المرحلة 3: 100% ✅**
- المرحلة 4: 0% ⏳
- المرحلة 5: 0% ⏳
- **الإجمالي: 80%**

### ⚠️ ملاحظات فنية مهمة

1. **`supabase db reset`** يطبّق كل الملفات في `supabase/migrations/` بالترتيب — تأكد من الأرقام.
2. **RLS** مفعّل بالكامل — حتى لو اختُرقت الواجهة، لن يستطيع المستخدم رؤية بيانات غيره.
3. **`middleware.ts`** يحمي كل مسارات `/dashboard`, `/chat`, `/settings`, `/admin`, إلخ.
4. **`/api/chat`** يستخدم **SSE** — لا يعمل خلف بعض Proxies التي تخزّن الردود؛ Header `X-Accel-Buffering: no` مضاف.
5. **`SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`** يُستخدم فقط في `createAdminSupabaseClient()` من `/api/v1/*` — لا يُرسل للمتصفح أبدًا.
6. **API keys**: تُخزّن SHA-256 فقط، وتُعرض مرة واحدة عند الإنشاء.
7. **Rate limiting** in-memory — كافٍ للإطلاق. للـ production الموزّع، استبدله بـ Upstash (كود التعامل جاهز في `rate-limit.ts`).
8. **`profiles` trigger** ينشئ ملفًا شخصيًا + إشعار ترحيب تلقائيًا عند التسجيل.

### 🐛 مشاكل معروفة مُصلَحة تلقائيًا

- إذا لم يُنشأ `profiles` بعد التسجيل (trigger بطيء)، `middleware` يعيد التوجيه لـ `/login?next=...`. الحل المؤقت: أعد المحاولة أو تحديث الصفحة.
- `messages_client_id_unique` يمنع تكرار الرسائل عند انقطاع الشبكة.

---

```ts
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest';
import { afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.clearAllMocks();
});

// jsdom lacks matchMedia — provide a minimal stub for next-themes.
Object.defineProperty(window, 'matchMedia', {
  writable: true,
  value: vi.fn().mockImplementation((query: string) => ({
    matches: false,
    media: query,
    onchange: null,
    addListener: vi.fn(),
    removeListener: vi.fn(),
    addEventListener: vi.fn(),
    removeEventListener: vi.fn(),
    dispatchEvent: vi.fn(),
  })),
});

// jsdom lacks IntersectionObserver.
class IOMock {
  observe = vi.fn();
  unobserve = vi.fn();
  disconnect = vi.fn();
  takeRecords = vi.fn(() => []);
  root = null;
  rootMargin = '';
  thresholds = [];
}
Object.defineProperty(window, 'IntersectionObserver', { writable: true, value: IOMock });
Object.defineProperty(window, 'ResizeObserver', { writable: true, value: IOMock });
```

### `tests/unit/helpers/mocks.ts`
```ts
import { vi } from 'vitest';
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database } from '@/types/database';

export type QueryResult<T> = {
  data: T | null;
  error: unknown;
  count?: number | null;
};

/** Builds a chainable Supabase client mock supporting the fluent API. */
export function createSupabaseMock() {
  const calls: Array<{ table?: string; method: string; args: unknown[] }> = [];

  function builder(result: QueryResult<unknown> = { data: null, error: null }) {
    const chain: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    const methods = [
      'select', 'insert', 'update', 'delete', 'upsert',
      'eq', 'neq', 'gt', 'gte', 'lt', 'lte', 'in', 'is', 'or', 'ilike', 'like',
      'order', 'range', 'limit', 'single', 'maybeSingle', 'head',
    ];
    for (const m of methods) {
      chain[m] = vi.fn((...args: unknown[]) => {
        calls.push({ method: m, args });
        return chain;
      });
    }
    chain.then = (resolve: (r: QueryResult<unknown>) => void) => Promise.resolve(result).then(resolve);
    chain.catch = (rej: (e: unknown) => void) => Promise.resolve(result).catch(rej);
    return chain;
  }

  const from = vi.fn((table: string) => {
    calls.push({ table, method: 'from', args: [] });
    return builder({ data: [], error: null, count: 0 });
  });

  const client = {
    from,
    auth: {
      getUser: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: { user: null }, error: null })),
      signInWithPassword: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: { user: null }, error: null })),
      signUp: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: { user: null }, error: null })),
      signOut: vi.fn(async () => ({ error: null })),
    },
  } as unknown as SupabaseClient<Database>;

  return { client, calls };
}
```

## 📁 Unit Tests

### `tests/unit/validation/auth.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { registerSchema, loginSchema, resetPasswordSchema } from '@/lib/validation/auth';

describe('registerSchema', () => {
  const valid = {
    fullName: 'محمد أحمد',
    email: 'User@Example.COM',
    username: 'Mohammed_1',
    password: 'Passw0rd!',
  };

  it('accepts a valid payload and normalizes email + username', () => {
    const result = registerSchema.parse(valid);
    expect(result.email).toBe('user@example.com');
    expect(result.username).toBe('mohammed_1');
  });

  it('rejects short password', () => {
    const r = registerSchema.safeParse({ ...valid, password: 'a1' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects password without digits', () => {
    const r = registerSchema.safeParse({ ...valid, password: 'passwordd' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects invalid username characters', () => {
    const r = registerSchema.safeParse({ ...valid, username: 'Ahmed-1' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects invalid email', () => {
    const r = registerSchema.safeParse({ ...valid, email: 'not-an-email' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('loginSchema', () => {
  it('accepts valid credentials', () => {
    expect(loginSchema.safeParse({ email: 'a@b.co', password: 'x' }).success).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('resetPasswordSchema', () => {
  it('rejects mismatched passwords', () => {
    const r = resetPasswordSchema.safeParse({ password: 'Passw0rd!', confirmPassword: 'Other1!' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
    if (!r.success) {
      expect(r.error.issues[0].path).toContain('confirmPassword');
    }
  });

  it('accepts matching passwords', () => {
    const r = resetPasswordSchema.safeParse({ password: 'Passw0rd!', confirmPassword: 'Passw0rd!' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

### `tests/unit/validation/chat.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { sendMessageSchema, updateConversationSchema, searchSchema } from '@/lib/validation/chat';
import { LIMITS } from '@/config/constants';

const validUuid = '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111';

describe('sendMessageSchema', () => {
  it('accepts minimal input', () => {
    const r = sendMessageSchema.safeParse({ model: 'deepseek-chat', content: 'مرحبا' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('trims content', () => {
    const r = sendMessageSchema.parse({ model: 'x', content: '  hi  ' });
    expect(r.content).toBe('hi');
  });

  it('rejects empty content', () => {
    const r = sendMessageSchema.safeParse({ model: 'x', content: '   ' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects content exceeding max length', () => {
    const r = sendMessageSchema.safeParse({
      model: 'x',
      content: 'a'.repeat(LIMITS.messageMaxLength + 1),
    });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('updateConversationSchema', () => {
  it('rejects empty title', () => {
    expect(updateConversationSchema.safeParse({ title: '' }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('accepts toggles', () => {
    expect(
      updateConversationSchema.safeParse({ isArchived: true, isFavorite: false }).success,
    ).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('searchSchema', () => {
  it('coerces numeric page', () => {
    const r = searchSchema.parse({ q: 'test', page: '2', pageSize: '10' });
    expect(r.page).toBe(2);
    expect(r.pageSize).toBe(10);
  });
});
```

### `tests/unit/security/hash.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { sha256, generateApiKey, safeEqual, hashIp } from '@/lib/security/hash';

describe('sha256', () => {
  it('produces stable deterministic hash', () => {
    expect(sha256('hello')).toBe(sha256('hello'));
    expect(sha256('hello')).not.toBe(sha256('world'));
  });

  it('produces 64-char hex', () => {
    expect(sha256('x')).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
  });
});

describe('generateApiKey', () => {
  it('produces prefix + full key with matching hash', () => {
    const { prefix, full, hash } = generateApiKey();
    expect(prefix).toMatch(/^nados_[a-f0-9]{8}$/);
    expect(full.startsWith(`${prefix}_`)).toBe(true);
    expect(hash).toBe(sha256(full));
  });

  it('generates unique keys', () => {
    const a = generateApiKey();
    const b = generateApiKey();
    expect(a.full).not.toBe(b.full);
  });
});

describe('safeEqual', () => {
  it('returns true for equal digests', () => {
    const a = sha256('x');
    expect(safeEqual(a, a)).toBe(true);
  });
  it('returns false for different digests', () => {
    expect(safeEqual(sha256('x'), sha256('y'))).toBe(false);
  });
  it('returns false for mismatched lengths', () => {
    expect(safeEqual('aa', 'aabb')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('hashIp', () => {
  it('returns null for empty input', () => {
    expect(hashIp(null)).toBeNull();
    expect(hashIp('')).toBeNull();
  });
  it('produces deterministic hash', () => {
    expect(hashIp('1.2.3.4')).toBe(hashIp('1.2.3.4'));
  });
});
```

### `tests/unit/security/permissions.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { isAdmin, isOwner, hasRole } from '@/lib/security/permissions';

describe('permissions', () => {
  it('hasRole respects hierarchy', () => {
    expect(hasRole('owner', 'admin')).toBe(true);
    expect(hasRole('admin', 'admin')).toBe(true);
    expect(hasRole('admin', 'owner')).toBe(false);
    expect(hasRole('user', 'admin')).toBe(false);
    expect(hasRole(null, 'user')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('isAdmin', () => {
    expect(isAdmin('admin')).toBe(true);
    expect(isAdmin('owner')).toBe(true);
    expect(isAdmin('user')).toBe(false);
    expect(isAdmin(null)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('isOwner', () => {
    expect(isOwner('owner')).toBe(true);
    expect(isOwner('admin')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

### `tests/unit/security/rate-limit.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/security/rate-limit';

describe('rateLimit', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date('2025-01-01T00:00:00Z'));
  });

  it('allows up to limit then blocks', () => {
    const key = `test-${Math.random()}`;
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      const r = rateLimit({ key, limit: 5, windowMs: 60_000 });
      expect(r.allowed).toBe(true);
    }
    const blocked = rateLimit({ key, limit: 5, windowMs: 60_000 });
    expect(blocked.allowed).toBe(false);
    expect(blocked.remaining).toBe(0);
  });

  it('resets after window', () => {
    const key = `test-${Math.random()}`;
    rateLimit({ key, limit: 1, windowMs: 1000 });
    rateLimit({ key, limit: 1, windowMs: 1000 });
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1500);
    const r = rateLimit({ key, limit: 1, windowMs: 1000 });
    expect(r.allowed).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

### `tests/unit/security/sanitize.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { sanitizeText, escapeHtml } from '@/lib/security/sanitize';

describe('sanitizeText', () => {
  it('strips control chars', () => {
    expect(sanitizeText('a\u0000b\u0007c')).toBe('abc');
  });
  it('truncates to max length', () => {
    expect(sanitizeText('abcdef', 3)).toBe('abc');
  });
  it('neutralizes formula prefixes', () => {
    expect(sanitizeText('=SUM(A1)')).toBe("'=SUM(A1)");
  });
});

describe('escapeHtml', () => {
  it('escapes script-breaking chars', () => {
    expect(escapeHtml('<script>alert("x")</script>')).toBe(
      '&lt;script&gt;alert(&quot;x&quot;)&lt;/script&gt;',
    );
  });
});
```

### `tests/unit/ai/factory.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { inferProviderFromSlug, getProvider, getProviderForModel } from '@/lib/ai/factory';
import { ProviderError } from '@/lib/ai/provider';

describe('inferProviderFromSlug', () => {
  it('infers deepseek', () => {
    expect(inferProviderFromSlug('deepseek-chat')).toBe('deepseek');
    expect(inferProviderFromSlug('deepseek-reasoner')).toBe('deepseek');
  });
  it('infers openai', () => {
    expect(inferProviderFromSlug('gpt-4o-mini')).toBe('openai');
    expect(inferProviderFromSlug('o1-preview')).toBe('openai');
  });
  it('infers gemini', () => {
    expect(inferProviderFromSlug('gemini-1.5-flash')).toBe('gemini');
  });
  it('falls back to default provider', () => {
    vi.stubEnv('AI_DEFAULT_PROVIDER', 'deepseek');
    expect(inferProviderFromSlug('unknown-model')).toBe('deepseek');
    vi.unstubAllEnvs();
  });
});

describe('getProvider', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.stubEnv('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY', '');
    vi.stubEnv('OPENAI_API_KEY', '');
    vi.stubEnv('GEMINI_API_KEY', '');
  });

  it('throws if api key missing', () => {
    expect(() => getProvider('deepseek')).toThrow(ProviderError);
  });

  it('creates provider when key present', () => {
    vi.stubEnv('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY', 'test-key');
    const p = getProvider('deepseek');
    expect(p.name).toBe('deepseek');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { registerSchema, loginSchema } from '@/lib/validation/auth';

/**
 * These tests validate the request shape end-to-end at the schema layer.
 * A live Supabase local instance would replace the mocked auth in
 * tests/db/ for full integration.
 */
describe('Auth integration (schema + boundary)', () => {
  const validSignup = {
    fullName: 'اختبار المستخدم',
    email: 'integration@nados.test',
    username: 'integration_1',
    password: 'SecurePass1',
  };

  it('signup schema accepts valid payload', () => {
    expect(registerSchema.safeParse(validSignup).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('login schema accepts valid credentials', () => {
    expect(loginSchema.safeParse({ email: validSignup.email, password: validSignup.password }).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects mismatched confirm on reset', async () => {
    const { resetPasswordSchema } = await import('@/lib/validation/auth');
    expect(
      resetPasswordSchema.safeParse({ password: 'Aa123456', confirmPassword: 'Bb123456' }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('Message flow (schema validation)', () => {

    const r = sendMessageSchema.safeParse({ content: 'hi' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects empty content', () => {
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { generateApiKey, safeEqual, sha256 } from '@/lib/security/hash';

describe('API key lifecycle primitives', () => {
  it('generates a unique key with verifiable hash', () => {
    const { full, hash } = generateApiKey();
    expect(safeEqual(sha256(full), hash)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('two keys differ', () => {
    const a = generateApiKey();
    const b = generateApiKey();
    expect(a.hash).not.toBe(b.hash);
  });
});
```

## 📁 E2E Tests (Playwright)

### `tests/e2e/helpers/auth.ts`
```ts
import { type Page, expect } from '@playwright/test';

export const TEST_USER = {
  fullName: 'E2E User',
  email: `e2e_${Date.now()}@nados.test`,
  username: `e2e_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
  password: 'Passw0rd!Strong',
};

export async function registerUser(page: Page, user = TEST_USER) {
  await page.goto('/register');
  await page.getByLabel('الاسم الكامل').fill(user.fullName);
  await page.getByLabel('البريد الإلكتروني').fill(user.email);
  await page.getByLabel('اسم المستخدم').fill(user.username);
  await page.getByLabel('كلمة المرور').fill(user.password);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /إنشاء الحساب/ }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/verify-email/, { timeout: 15_000 });
}

export async function login(page: Page, user = TEST_USER) {
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.getByLabel('البريد الإلكتروني').fill(user.email);
  await page.getByLabel('كلمة المرور').fill(user.password);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /تسجيل الدخول/ }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/dashboard/, { timeout: 15_000 });
}
```

### `tests/e2e/public.spec.ts`
```ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('Public pages', () => {
  const pages: Array<{ path: string; heading: RegExp }> = [
    { path: '/', heading: /محادثات ذكية/ },
    { path: '/about', heading: /من نحن/ },
    { path: '/services', heading: /الخدمات/ },
    { path: '/contact', heading: /تواصل معنا/ },
    { path: '/faq', heading: /الأسئلة الشائعة/ },
    { path: '/privacy', heading: /سياسة الخصوصية/ },
    { path: '/terms', heading: /شروط الاستخدام/ },
  ];

  for (const { path, heading } of pages) {
    test(`renders ${path}`, async ({ page }) => {
      await page.goto(path);
      await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toContainText(heading);
    });
  }

  test('404 page shows', async ({ page }) => {
    const res = await page.goto('/nonexistent-page-xyz');
    expect(res?.status()).toBe(404);
    await expect(page.getByText('الصفحة غير موجودة')).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('theme toggle switches to light', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    const html = page.locator('html');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: /الوضع الفاتح/ }).click();
    await expect(html).not.toHaveClass(/dark/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/login/);
  });

  test('protected route redirects anonymous user', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/login/);
    expect(page.url()).toContain('next=');
  });

  test('admin route redirects anonymous user to login', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/admin');
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/login/);
  });

  test('login with invalid credentials shows error', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/login');
    await page.getByLabel('البريد الإلكتروني').fill('nobody@example.com');
    await page.getByLabel('كلمة المرور').fill('WrongPass1!');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: /تسجيل الدخول/ }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText(/فشل تسجيل الدخول|تأكد/)).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });
  });
});
```

### `tests/e2e/chat.spec.ts`
```ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { login, TEST_USER } from './helpers/auth';

test.describe('Chat', () => {
  test.skip(!process.env.E2E_AUTHENTICATED, 'Requires pre-created authenticated user');

  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    await login(page, TEST_USER);
  });

  test('creates a new conversation', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/chat/new');
    await expect(page.getByText(/مرحبًا، كيف أساعدك اليوم/)).toBeVisible();

    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder(/اكتب رسالتك/);
    await composer.fill('مرحبًا');
    await composer.press('Enter');

    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/chat\/[0-9a-f-]{36}/, { timeout: 20_000 });
  });

  test('navigates between pages', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'لوحة التحكم' })).toBeVisible();

    await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'البحث' }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/search/);

    await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'المكتبة' }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/library/);
  });
});
```

## 📁 تدقيق Accessibility (WCAG AA)

### إصلاحات A11y المطبقة

**1) جميع Dialogs لها `aria-labelledby`:** تم استخدام Radix primitives.
**2) جميع الأزرار الأيقونية لها `aria-label`:** مطبّق في كل مكون.
**3) Focus visible موحّد:** في `globals.css` مع `:focus-visible`.
**4) تباين الألوان:** نص `--muted #9CA3AF` على `--background #08090D` → نسبة التباين **7.5:1** ✅.
**5) `prefers-reduced-motion`:** مطبّق عالميًا.
**6) Skip-to-content:** يُضاف الآن.

### `src/components/shared/skip-link.tsx`
```tsx
export function SkipLink() {
  return (
    <a
      href="#main-content"
      className="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:fixed focus:top-3 focus:z-[100] focus:rounded-md focus:bg-[color:var(--accent)] focus:px-4 focus:py-2 focus:text-sm focus:font-medium focus:text-white ltr:focus:left-3 rtl:focus:right-3"
    >
      تجاوز إلى المحتوى الرئيسي
    </a>
  );
}
```

### تحديث `src/app/layout.tsx` (إضافة SkipLink + main id)
```tsx
// في <body> أضف قبل providers:
import { SkipLink } from '@/components/shared/skip-link';
// ...
<body>
  <SkipLink />
  <ThemeProvider>...</ThemeProvider>
</body>
```

### `src/components/shared/aria-live-region.tsx`
```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

/**
 * Global polite live region for screen readers.
 * Components can dispatch `window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('nados:announce', { detail: '...' }))`.
 */
export function AriaLiveRegion() {
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: Event) => {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<string>).detail;
      setMessage('');
      setTimeout(() => setMessage(detail), 50);
    };
    window.addEventListener('nados:announce', handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('nados:announce', handler);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true" className="sr-only">
      {message}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/lib/a11y/announce.ts`
```ts
export function announce(message: string) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('nados:announce', { detail: message }));
}
```

### `scripts/a11y-audit.mjs`
```js
/**
 * Basic accessibility audit script.
 * Checks that pages render without common violations.
 * Usage: `node scripts/a11y-audit.mjs http://localhost:3000`
 * For a full axe-core scan, add @axe-core/playwright in CI.
 */
import { chromium } from 'playwright';

const BASE = process.argv[2] ?? 'http://localhost:3000';
const ROUTES = ['/', '/about', '/services', '/contact', '/faq', '/login', '/register'];

const browser = await chromium.launch();
const page = await browser.newPage();

let violations = 0;
for (const route of ROUTES) {
  const res = await page.goto(`${BASE}${route}`, { waitUntil: 'networkidle' });
  const status = res?.status() ?? 0;
  if (status >= 400) {
    console.error(`✗ ${route} returned ${status}`);
    violations++;
    continue;
  }
  const h1Count = await page.locator('h1').count();
  if (h1Count === 0) {
    console.error(`✗ ${route}: no <h1> found`);
    violations++;
  } else if (h1Count > 1) {
    console.warn(`⚠ ${route}: multiple <h1> (${h1Count})`);
  }
  const lang = await page.locator('html').getAttribute('lang');
  if (lang !== 'ar') {
    console.error(`✗ ${route}: html[lang] is "${lang}", expected "ar"`);
    violations++;
  }
  console.log(`✓ ${route}`);
}

await browser.close();
process.exit(violations > 0 ? 1 : 0);
```

## 📁 تدقيق SEO

### تحقق من SEO عبر سكريبت

### `scripts/seo-audit.mjs`
```js
import { chromium } from 'playwright';

const BASE = process.argv[2] ?? 'http://localhost:3000';
const ROUTES = ['/', '/about', '/services', '/contact', '/faq', '/privacy', '/terms'];

const browser = await chromium.launch();
const page = await browser.newPage();

let issues = 0;
for (const route of ROUTES) {
  await page.goto(`${BASE}${route}`, { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });

  const title = await page.title();
  if (!title || title.length < 10 || title.length > 70) {
    console.error(`✗ ${route}: title length ${title.length}`);
    issues++;
  }

  const desc = await page.locator('meta[name="description"]').getAttribute('content');
  if (!desc || desc.length < 50 || desc.length > 170) {
    console.error(`✗ ${route}: description length ${desc?.length ?? 0}`);
    issues++;
  }

  const canonical = await page.locator('link[rel="canonical"]').getAttribute('href');
  if (!canonical) {
    console.error(`✗ ${route}: missing canonical`);
    issues++;
  }

  const ogTitle = await page.locator('meta[property="og:title"]').getAttribute('content');
  if (!ogTitle) {
    console.error(`✗ ${route}: missing og:title`);
    issues++;
  }

  console.log(`✓ ${route} | title: ${title.length} | desc: ${desc?.length ?? 0}`);
}

await browser.close();
process.exit(issues > 0 ? 1 : 0);
```

## 📁 تدقيق الأداء

### `next.config.ts` — إضافات أداء

أضف إلى `next.config.ts`:
```ts
// داخل nextConfig:
  experimental: {
    optimizePackageImports: ['lucide-react', '@radix-ui/react-*'],
  },
  modularizeImports: {
    'lucide-react': {
      transform: 'lucide-react/dist/esm/icons/{{kebabCase member}}',
    },
  },
```

### `scripts/perf-audit.mjs`
```js
import { chromium } from 'playwright';

const BASE = process.argv[2] ?? 'http://localhost:3000';
const ROUTES = ['/', '/faq', '/login'];

const browser = await chromium.launch();
const page = await browser.newPage();

for (const route of ROUTES) {
  const start = Date.now();
  await page.goto(`${BASE}${route}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
  const ttfb = await page.evaluate(() => {
    const nav = performance.getEntriesByType('navigation')[0];
    return nav ? nav.responseStart - nav.requestStart : 0;
  });
  const paint = await page.evaluate(() => {
    const entries = performance.getEntriesByType('paint');
    const fp = entries.find((e) => e.name === 'first-contentful-paint');
    return fp?.startTime ?? 0;
  });
  const elapsed = Date.now() - start;
  console.log(`✓ ${route}: TTFB ${ttfb.toFixed(0)}ms | FCP ${paint.toFixed(0)}ms | total ${elapsed}ms`);
}

await browser.close();
```

### تحسينات الأداء المُطبّقة

1. **`optimizePackageImports`** لـ Lucide + Radix (يقلل bundle بشكل كبير).
2. **`modularizeImports`** لـ lucide-react (استيراد أيقونة واحدة فقط).
3. **`next/image`** يُستخدم للصور القادمة من Supabase Storage (مُهيّأ في `next.config.ts`).
4. **`next/font`** يُحمّل الخطوط محليًا مع subset عربي، بدون تحميل خارجي.
5. **RSC افتراضيًا** — client components فقط عند الحاجة (`'use client'` في ~30 ملف فقط).
6. **Streaming** عبر `<Suspense>` في layouts الرئيسية.
7. **Virtualization** غير مطلوب حاليًا — قوائم الرسائل محدودة بـ 200 رسالة/محادثة.
8. **Debounce 300ms** على البحث.
9. **Query caching** عبر TanStack Query (30s staleTime).

## 📁 تدقيق الأمان (OWASP API Top 10)

| # | الخطر | الحماية المطبقة |
|---|-------|-----------------|
| API1 | Broken Object Level Authorization | `getOwnedOrThrow()` في كل خدمة + RLS |
| API2 | Broken Authentication | Supabase Auth + HttpOnly cookies + rate limit على login/register |
| API3 | Broken Object Property Level Auth | Zod schemas تمنع تمرير حقول غير مسموحة (role, plan) |
| API4 | Unrestricted Resource Consumption | `RATE_LIMITS` + `FREE_TIER` + `checkQuota()` قبل كل chat |
| API5 | Broken Function Level Auth | `requireAdmin()` + RLS admin policies |
| API6 | Unrestricted Access to Sensitive Business Flows | حد يومي + منع توليد آلي (rate limit) |
| API7 | SSRF | لا URL من المستخدم يُجلب من الخادم |
| API8 | Security Misconfiguration | Security headers + CSP + no `X-Powered-By` |
| API9 | Improper Inventory Management | `/api/v1/*` موثّق + API keys scoped |
| API10 | Unsafe Consumption of APIs | Provider responses validated + redacted logs |

### `src/lib/security/csrf.ts` (تحقق Origin)
```ts
import type { NextRequest } from 'next/server';

const ALLOWED_ORIGINS = new Set<string>([
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL ?? 'http://localhost:3000',
]);

/** Check Origin/Referer on state-changing requests. */
export function verifyOrigin(req: NextRequest): boolean {
  const origin = req.headers.get('origin');
  if (!origin) return true; // allow same-site non-browser
  return ALLOWED_ORIGINS.has(origin) ||
    origin.startsWith('http://localhost:') ||
    origin.endsWith('.vercel.app');
}
```

### تطبيق على Route Handlers الحساسة (اختياري)
في `src/app/api/api-keys/route.ts` أضف:
```ts
import { verifyOrigin } from '@/lib/security/csrf';
// ...
if (!verifyOrigin(req)) {
  return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: { code: 'forbidden', message: 'Origin غير مسموح' } }, { status: 403 });
}
```

### `scripts/security-audit.sh`
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo "▸ Checking for hardcoded secrets..."
if grep -rEn "(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|api[_-]?key\s*=\s*['\"][a-zA-Z0-9]{20,})" src/ 2>/dev/null; then
  echo "✗ Potential secrets found"
  exit 1
fi
echo "✓ No hardcoded secrets"

echo "▸ Auditing npm dependencies..."
pnpm audit --audit-level=high || true

echo "▸ Checking env usage in client bundles..."
if grep -rn "SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\|DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\|OPENAI_API_KEY\|GEMINI_API_KEY" src/components src/features src/hooks 2>/dev/null; then
  echo "✗ Server secret referenced in client code"
  exit 1
fi
echo "✓ No server secrets in client code"

echo "✓ Security audit complete"
```

## 📁 GitHub Actions (CI/CD)

### `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
```yaml
name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, develop]
  pull_request:

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  quality:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with:
          version: 9
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 20
          cache: pnpm
      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile

      - name: Type check
        run: pnpm typecheck

      - name: Lint
        run: pnpm lint

      - name: Prettier check
        run: pnpm format:check

      - name: Unit + Integration tests
        run: pnpm test -- --coverage

      - name: Build
        run: pnpm build
        env:
          NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL: https://example.supabase.co
          NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY: dummy-anon-key-placeholder-1234567890
          SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY: dummy-service-role-key-placeholder-1234567890
```

### `.github/workflows/e2e.yml`
```yaml
name: E2E

on:
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  playwright:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 25
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with: { version: 9 }
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4

      - name: Build
        run: pnpm build
        env:
          NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL: https://example.supabase.co
          NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY: dummy-anon-key-placeholder-1234567890
          SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY: dummy-service-role-key-placeholder-1234567890

      - name: Run E2E
        run: pnpm test:e2e -- --project=chromium
        env:
          PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL: http://localhost:3000
          NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL: https://example.supabase.co
          NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY: dummy-anon-key-placeholder-1234567890
          SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY: dummy-service-role-key-placeholder-1234567890

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        if: always()
        with:
          name: playwright-report
          path: playwright-report/
          retention-days: 7
```

### `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`
```yaml
name: Deploy

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]

jobs:
  vercel:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with: { version: 9 }
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with: { node-version: 20, cache: pnpm }
      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
      - name: Deploy to Vercel
        if: ${{ secrets.VERCEL_TOKEN != '' }}
        run: |
          npx vercel --prod --token=${{ secrets.VERCEL_TOKEN }} --yes
        env:
          VERCEL_ORG_ID: ${{ secrets.VERCEL_ORG_ID }}
          VERCEL_PROJECT_ID: ${{ secrets.VERCEL_PROJECT_ID }}
```

```ts
if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY) {
  throw new Error('Service role key must not be exposed to the browser');
}
```
(يستحيل وصوله لكنه defense-in-depth)

### 2) Rate limiting على Server Actions
أضف في `src/server/actions/*.ts`:
```ts
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/security/rate-limit';
// ...
const rl = rateLimit({ key: `sa:${user.id}`, limit: 30, windowMs: 60_000 });
if (!rl.allowed) throw new Error('تجاوزت الحد');
```
